# Methods: magpie/cwplans

Generated by `tools/check-data-register.mjs --write` on 2026-10-04 from `methods-intro.md` (hand-written), `pipeline.json` (one activity per tool, 89 activities, and 14 hand steps), the fault register in `skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md` and `quality/issues.json`. Edit those files, not this one.

Contents: policy and owner rules; methods by subject area, in rebuild order; hand-judgement steps; fault register; rebuild order; libraries and tests.

## Policy and owner rules that govern the methods

This section is hand-written (`methods-intro.md`). `tools/check-data-register.mjs --write` puts it at the top of
`METHODS.md`; everything after it is generated from `pipeline.json`.

Owner instruction, 2026-10-03: "record ALL our data methods in skills or other concrete committed artifacts". So every
method of this project is in one of three places: a tool's activity in `pipeline.json` (method, rules, inputs,
outputs, network, order), a hand step in `pipeline.json` `manual_activities` (what a person or an agent decided, and
where the decision is written), or this section (the rules that govern all of them).

### The rules, with their source and date

| rule | source and date | what it means for a method |
|---|---|---|
| Personal, organisation and address data allowed here, for this phase only | Repo `CLAUDE.md`, Data ethics, "EXCEPTION — magpie/cwplans/" (owner, October 2026: "This is a special project and we will be suspending our normal restrictions on personal org and address data while in the scoping, planning and prototyping phase.") | Company addresses, occupant and owner records, postcode-level business and home data may be fetched, joined and committed in `magpie/cwplans/` only. The exception ends when the owner says the project leaves prototyping. |
| No proprietary, restricted-licence or virally licensed data | `CLAUDE.md`, same exception | Read the licence of each source before a file is committed. The VOA rating list is named as restricted: `registry-voa.mjs` output stays local. |
| OpenStreetMap (ODbL) allowed for now, and tracked | `CLAUDE.md` (owner, 2026-10-03: "ODbL is ok for now, and will be thoroughly reviewed as part of the planning and prototyping activities later. Keep track of our use of this data carefully.") | Every committed file says how it uses OSM (`data-register.json` `osm.use`) and from which extract; every page that shows OSM data carries "© OpenStreetMap contributors" linked to https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright. No other share-alike source without the owner's agreement. |
| Website crawls are fair use for scoping | `CLAUDE.md` (owner, 2026-10-03: "Website crawls - direct and via IA or CommonCrawl etc are fair use for our scoping purposes.") | Pages fetched directly, from the Internet Archive or from Common Crawl, and data taken from them, are allowed. Record each crawl in the data register with its method and date. Re-check before anything leaves prototyping. |
| Store-finder searches with a UK postcode | Curation skill (owner, 2026-10-03: "we are permitted per industry convention to submit storefinder forms with UK postcodes") | A crawl may type a UK postcode into a brand's store-finder search and follow the result to the branch page. Nothing else: no other forms, no names, emails, accounts or bookings, no login. robots.txt and the per-host gap still apply. Record how each page was reached (search, postcode, finder URL). |
| Every committed data file is registered | `CLAUDE.md`, same exception | Add the `data-register.json` entry in the same commit as the file, then run `node magpie/cwplans/tools/check-data-register.mjs --write`. |
| Every tool has its method written down | Owner, 2026-10-03 (above); curation skill, "Methods" | A new or changed tool updates its `pipeline.json` activity in the same commit. The check fails on an unlisted tool and on an empty method or rules. |
| Keep sources ready for a knowledge graph | Curation skill (owner, 2026-10-03: "In future we will create a corpus that brings select sources through a data integration pipeline into a Knowledge Graph environment. Bear that in mind as we record sources and transforms.") | Keep source identifiers as they come (OSM type/id, Wikidata QID, UPRN, TOID, FHRS id, company number, cwb- id); keep dates and qualifiers with values; `pipeline.jsonld` exports the activities as W3C PROV-O. |
| Catalogue error classes first | Curation skill (owner, October 2026) | A wrong value gets an error class and a check in `tools/audit-quality.mjs`; the record is not patched by hand. A tool changes only for a fault in our own pipeline or join rule, and the audit counts before and after go in the activity log. |
| Pace our own requests | Curation skill (owner, 2026-10-03: "maybe the rate limiting should come from us? Or a more carefully posed efficient query? QLever is very good") | One well-posed query (VALUES over the ids) in place of many small ones; `tools/lib.mjs` `qlever()` runs one query at a time, at least 1.5 s apart, with backoff on 429 and 5xx. Every crawler identifies itself (User-Agent `glitchcan-cwplans/0.1 (https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam)`) and keeps a per-host gap. |
| Memorial and burial content is not material | `CLAUDE.md`, Data ethics (Bristol trees rule; June 2026 incident) | The trees payload keeps only position, taxon, height, crown, source and record id. Wikidata memorials, graves, cemeteries, tombs and plaques are dropped; people (Q5) are not owners or occupants. Museum records flagged "sensitive" are left out of the published subset. EA flood-defence owner and maintainer fields are dropped. |
| Lessons go in skills, not in code comments | `CLAUDE.md` (owner, August 2026: "Don't store your lessons in code, use agentskills with frontmatter.") | A tool header says what the tool does and how to run it; the reasons and the measured lessons are in the curation skill; the method is in `pipeline.json`. |
| Ship at once | Curation skill (owner, 2026-10-03) | Commit and push each working change as soon as its check passes; regenerate this file in the same commit. |

### Crawl and store-finder method (all crawlers)

- Identify: User-Agent `glitchcan-cwplans/0.1 (https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam)`; the headless render adds
  that token to a desktop Chrome string.
- robots.txt per RFC 9309: the group for `glitchcan-cwplans`, else `*`; longest match wins, Allow wins a tie; a 4xx
  robots.txt allows all; a 5xx or unreachable robots.txt disallows all; checked again on every redirect to a new host.
- One request at a time per host with a gap (1.5 s for the plain crawl, 2 s for the headless render, about 1 s for the
  store-page check), a few hosts at once, a timeout, and backoff on 429, 5xx, timeouts and resets (Retry-After first).
- Skipped by rule: FSA ratings pages, OSM, Wikidata, Companies House and Land Registry pages (their data comes from
  APIs and bulk files), Wikipedia (CC BY-SA, share-alike), social networks, and the live canarywharf.com (it answers
  scripts with an Imperva challenge; its pages are read as Internet Archive `id_` copies).
- Kept: structured data (schema.org JSON-LD, microdata, RDFa), titles, OpenGraph fields cut to 200 characters,
  links, feeds. No other page text. Rendered pages stay local (gitignored).
- A page counts for a branch only when it names the place (postcode, street, mall); a result that does not is reported
  as such, not dropped silently.

### Hand curation (general rules)

- A hand decision is written to a file the tools read (`fsa-decisions.json`, `cwg-decisions.json`,
  `storelocator-manual.json`, `data/sourced-levels.json`, `registry/sources/facades/work/rois.json`, the `JUDGEMENT`
  table in `tools/build-towers.py`), with its reason. The tools apply it on every run; nobody edits an output by hand.
- Research catalogues (`feeds/`, `feeds/underground/`, `docklands/facts.json`) keep the URL, the fetch date, the
  licence as read, and a short quote where a value is taken. Copyright plans are looked at, not copied.
- Where a method was run as a one-off script that is not committed, its hand step in `pipeline.json` says so
  (`gap`). Commit the script next time the step is run.

### Licence decisions

The licence of each source is in `data-register.json` `sources` (readable in `DATA-REGISTER.md`), with the reason
for each decision in the source's README. The open decisions and refusals are in the hand step `licence-decisions`
below (area "Provenance export").

## Methods by subject area

Each tool activity gives its command, method, rules, inputs, outputs, network and determinism, and the activities that must run before it. The number is its place in the rebuild order. A hand step is a method that is not a tool. "local" files are not committed (raw downloads, caches, excluded outputs).

### 1. Terrain and LiDAR

#### 2. `fetch-raw-grid` (tools/fetch-raw.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-raw.mjs grid`
- Method: Downloads the OSTN15 grid that every WGS84 to British National Grid conversion in the project uses (tools/lib.mjs bngProjector).
- Rules:
  - The file is written as fetched; no change.
  - Network: one GET with User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; no retry; the file is overwritten on every run (no cache).
- Inputs:
  - **os-ostn15** (Ordnance Survey OSTN15 transformation grid, free to use as published by OS; licence text to be recorded before production): https://cdn.proj.org/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif; the OS OSTN15 NTv2 transformation grid (GeoTIFF) as distributed by the PROJ CDN
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing

#### 4. `fetch-raw-lidar` (tools/fetch-raw.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-raw.mjs lidar`
- Method: Downloads one DTM and one first-return DSM GeoTIFF (EA LiDAR Composite, 1 m) over the corridor box by WCS 2.0.1 GetCoverage.
- Rules:
  - BNG box E 534600-536300, N 178300-180500.
  - Coverages: 13787b9a-26a4-4775-8523-806d13af58fc__Lidar_Composite_Elevation_DTM_1m and df4e3ec3-315e-48aa-aaaf-b5ae74d7b2bb__Lidar_Composite_Elevation_FZ_DSM_1m; heights in metres above Ordnance Datum Newlyn.
  - Network: two GETs in sequence, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; no retry; files overwritten on every run.
- Inputs:
  - **ea-lidar** (Environment Agency LiDAR Composite DTM/DSM 1 m, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/spatialdata/lidar-composite-digital-terrain-model-dtm-1m/wcs; WCS 2.0.1 GetCoverage, DTM 1 m, GeoTIFF, subset E(534600,536300) N(178300,180500)
  - **ea-lidar** (Environment Agency LiDAR Composite DTM/DSM 1 m, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/spatialdata/lidar-composite-digital-surface-model-first-return-dsm-1m/wcs; WCS 2.0.1 GetCoverage, first-return DSM 1 m, GeoTIFF, same subset
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/dtm.tif` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/dsm.tif` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing

#### 7. `fetch-docklands-lidar` (tools/fetch-docklands.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-docklands.mjs lidar`
- Method: Downloads the DTM and DSM of the Docklands box as 1 km tiles (48 tiles each).
- Rules:
  - A tile is fetched only when its file is missing or smaller than 1000 bytes.
  - Four downloads run at a time; each is tried up to 4 times with a growing pause.
  - Tile edge 1000 m (TILE); a tile at the box edge is cut to the box. Heights in metres above Ordnance Datum Newlyn.
  - Pause before a retry: 2000 x 2^attempt ms (4, 8, 16 s); User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1.
- Inputs:
  - **ea-lidar** (Environment Agency LiDAR Composite DTM/DSM 1 m, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/spatialdata/lidar-composite-digital-terrain-model-dtm-1m/wcs; WCS 2.0.1 GetCoverage, DTM 1 m, one GeoTIFF per 1 km tile of BNG E 532400-539900, N 176700-182300
  - **ea-lidar** (Environment Agency LiDAR Composite DTM/DSM 1 m, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/spatialdata/lidar-composite-digital-surface-model-first-return-dsm-1m/wcs; WCS 2.0.1 GetCoverage, first-return DSM 1 m, same tiles
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/docklands/lidar/dtm_<E>_<N>.tif` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/docklands/lidar/dsm_<E>_<N>.tif` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing

#### 43. `fetch-dsm` (tools/fetch-dsm.sh)

- Command: `bash magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-dsm.sh`
- Method: Downloads the EA LiDAR surface model tiles over the Canary Wharf estate for every survey year; skips files already present.
- Rules:
  - 2012 TQ3580 exists only at 0.5 m; the 1 m request returns a server error, not a ZIP
  - a ZIP already present is not fetched again
  - Network: curl in sequence, 1200 s timeout, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; a failed download prints FAIL and the loop goes on; no retry.
- Inputs:
  - **ea-dsm-history** (Environment Agency LiDAR DSM survey tiles 1999 to 2022, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/tiles/collections/survey/<product>/<year>/<res>/<tile>; GET a ZIP per product, year and tile: lidar_tiles_dsm 1999 (2 m), 2003 (1 m), 2007 (0.5 m), 2012 (1 m and 0.5 m), 2015 (1 m); national_lidar_programme_dsm 2018 and 2020 (1 m); lidar_composite_first_return_dsm 2022 (1 m); tiles TQ3575 and TQ3580
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/dsm/<product>-<year>-<res>-<tile>.zip` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing

### 2. Buildings and heights (OSM extracts and the 3D model builds)

#### 3. `fetch-raw-osm` (tools/fetch-raw.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-raw.mjs osm`
- Method: Fetches the OSM map call for the small corridor box, then the full geometry of water multipolygons whose member ways the map call leaves out (the Pool of London).
- Rules:
  - Box WGS84 west -0.0620, south 51.4880, east -0.0380, north 51.5070.
  - A relation is fetched in full when it is type=multipolygon, natural=water and has a member way that is not in the map call.
  - Network: one map call, then one /full call per incomplete water relation, in sequence; User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; no retry; files overwritten on every run.
  - Stored gzipped (level 9); the JSON content is as returned.
- Inputs:
  - **osm** (OpenStreetMap, ODbL 1.0): https://api.openstreetmap.org/api/0.6/map.json?bbox=-0.062,51.488,-0.038,51.507; OSM API map call for the corridor box (Canada Water)
  - **osm** (OpenStreetMap, ODbL 1.0): https://api.openstreetmap.org/api/0.6/relation/{id}/full.json; full member lists of natural=water multipolygons that have member ways outside the box
- Outputs:
  - [data/raw/osm-map.json.gz](data/raw/osm-map.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/osm-water-full.json.gz](data/raw/osm-water-full.json.gz)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Faults: [F10](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)
- Note: fetch-raw.mjs is also imported as a module by build-data.mjs (BBOX_BNG).

#### 5. `fetch-raw-wikidata` (tools/fetch-raw.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-raw.mjs wikidata`
- Method: Stores two Wikidata query results as returned: items near Canada Water and selected facts for named items.
- Rules:
  - Results are written as the endpoint returns them.
  - Network: two POSTs to query.wikidata.org in sequence, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; no retry; no cache.
- Inputs:
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://query.wikidata.org/sparql; SPARQL: wikibase:around Point(-0.0497 51.4975) radius 1.3 km: item, label, description, coordinate, distance, P31 class, P571 inception, English Wikipedia article
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://query.wikidata.org/sparql; SPARQL: statements P31, P571, P1619, P2043, P2044, P2048, P1448, P576, P2046, P127, P137, P1435 (with units) of 12 fixed items (Q1190691, Q800610, Q801499, Q175256, Q801580, Q1032006, Q2368643, Q1544727, Q128924146, Q26665615, Q7604796, Q17642970)
- Outputs:
  - [data/raw/wikidata-around.json](data/raw/wikidata-around.json)
  - [data/raw/wikidata-facts.json](data/raw/wikidata-facts.json)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing

#### 6. `fetch-docklands-osm` (tools/fetch-docklands.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-docklands.mjs osm`
- Method: Downloads the Greater London OSM extract that every OSM clip and scan in the project reads.
- Rules:
  - The file is written as fetched.
  - Network: one GET (about 150 MB), User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; no retry; overwritten on every run. The extract date is the osmosis_replication_timestamp in the PBF header (data-register.json osm_extracts).
- Inputs:
  - **osm** (OpenStreetMap, ODbL 1.0): https://download.openstreetmap.fr/extracts/europe/united_kingdom/england/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf; the daily Greater London extract (data register osm_extracts: osmfr-greater-london)
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Note: fetch-docklands.mjs is also imported as a module (DIR, BOX_WGS84, BOX_BNG, ORIGIN, lidarTiles, tileFile) by osm-clip-docklands, build-docklands, postcodes, registry-osm, build-registry, build-atlas, build-indoor, build-imagery and build-skyline.

#### 9. `fetch-docklands-wikidata` (tools/fetch-docklands.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-docklands.mjs wikidata`
- Method: Stores two Wikidata query results for the Docklands box, gzipped as returned.
- Rules:
  - Results are written as the endpoint returns them.
  - Network: two POSTs to query.wikidata.org in sequence, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; no retry; no cache; stored gzipped (level 9).
- Inputs:
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://query.wikidata.org/sparql; SPARQL: wikibase:box Point(-0.0950 51.4740) to Point(0.0150 51.5220): every item with P625, its P31 classes, English label, description and English Wikipedia article
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://query.wikidata.org/sparql; SPARQL: same box; statements P2048, P1101, P1139, P2044, P2043, P4511, P2610, P571, P1619, P2046 with their units
- Outputs:
  - [data/raw/docklands/wikidata-items.json.gz](data/raw/docklands/wikidata-items.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/docklands/wikidata-facts.json.gz](data/raw/docklands/wikidata-facts.json.gz)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing

#### 17. `osm-clip-docklands` (tools/osm-clip-docklands.mjs)

- Command: `node --max-old-space-size=6000 magpie/cwplans/tools/osm-clip-docklands.mjs`
- Method: Streams the Greater London PBF three times and keeps the ways, relations and points the Docklands model uses, with their node coordinates.
- Rules:
  - Node box: the Docklands box plus a 0.004 degree margin (about 300 m).
  - Ways kept (KEEP_WAY): buildings and parts, water, railways, main roads, tunnels, bridges, indoor and level-tagged highways, underground parking, piers and shafts, platforms, parks and greens, named places.
  - Relations kept: multipolygon or building relations with a kept member way; their member ways outside the box are pulled in whole.
  - Points kept: stations and entrances, named places, shafts, entrances with a level, piers; and in the Canary Wharf focus box (-0.0300, 51.4980 to -0.0050, 51.5100) every node with shop, amenity, level, indoor or name.
  - Only tags that match the TAG list are kept.
  - A way that touches the box is kept whole; its nodes outside the box come from a second pass.
  - Node coordinates rounded to 7 decimals (the OSM precision).
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/docklands/osm-clip.json.gz` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: clip; after: `fetch-docklands-osm`

#### 22. `build-data` (tools/build-data.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/build-data.mjs`
- Method: Builds the corridor model (Canada Water): a 10 m terrain grid, building footprints with heights, water levels, railways with tunnel chains and open cuts, roads, and stations and Wikidata places, in local metres around E0 535400, N0 179400.
- Rules:
  - Terrain: median of the DTM in a 7 x 7 m window every 10 m.
  - Building height: OSM height tag; else the 90th percentile of DSM - DTM inside the footprint (at least 6 cells, at least 2 m) unless it is under half of building:levels x 3 m; else building:levels x 3 m + 1 m ('newer' when LiDAR exists); else the LiDAR value; else 6 m (0 for building=construction).
  - Buildings: centroid inside the box with a 2 m margin; single-outer multipolygons only.
  - Water: natural=water, waterway=dock or landuse=basin over 50 m2; level = 25th percentile of the DTM inside.
  - Tunnel portals: tunnel nodes shared with an open railway way, at the open track level (DSM on bridges, DTM otherwise).
  - Open cut: a tunnel point where the DTM is more than 6 m below the ground 25-35 m around; each run of cut points takes its lowest bed.
  - Published levels from data/sourced-levels.json are applied to the nearest tunnel point within 150 m (lib.mjs applyControls); superseded controls are skipped.
  - Places: OSM railway=station nodes, then Wikidata items of a fixed list of transport, water and landform classes that are not already a station.
  - Coordinates: WGS84 to BNG through OSTN15 (tools/lib.mjs bngProjector); local metres x = E - 535400, z = -(N - 179400); heights in metres above Ordnance Datum Newlyn.
  - Rounding: geometry in whole decimetres, delta-encoded (tools/lib.mjs enc); heights and levels to 0.1 m (r1); control levels to 0.01 m.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/dtm.tif` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/dsm.tif` (local, not committed)
  - [data/raw/osm-map.json.gz](data/raw/osm-map.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/osm-water-full.json.gz](data/raw/osm-water-full.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/wikidata-around.json](data/raw/wikidata-around.json)
  - [data/raw/wikidata-facts.json](data/raw/wikidata-facts.json)
  - [data/sourced-levels.json](data/sourced-levels.json)
- Outputs:
  - [data/cwplans-data.js](data/cwplans-data.js)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: derive; after: `fetch-raw-grid`, `fetch-raw-osm`, `fetch-raw-lidar`, `fetch-raw-wikidata`
- Hand judgement: The published levels in data/sourced-levels.json are chosen by hand (manual activity sourced-levels).
- Faults: [F10](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F13](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 23. `build-docklands` (tools/build-docklands.mjs)

- Command: `node --max-old-space-size=8000 magpie/cwplans/tools/build-docklands.mjs`
- Method: Builds the Docklands 3D model (terrain, water, greens, buildings and parts, roads, railways, tunnels, places, flood defences, riverbed) into area.js, and the Canary Wharf underground detail (basements, indoor ways, level-tagged points, published structures) into under.js, in local metres around E0 537550, N0 180300.
- Rules:
  - Terrain: median of the DTM in a 7 x 7 m window every 20 m.
  - Building height: OSM height; else LiDAR 90th percentile of DSM - DTM (at least 6 cells, at least 2 m) unless under half of building:levels x 3 m; else Wikidata P2048 (outlines only); else building:levels x 3 m + 1 m ('newer' when LiDAR exists); else LiDAR; else 6 m (0 for construction, roof, ruins).
  - An outline that contains building parts is drawn by its parts; a part's base is its min_height, else building:min_level x 3 m.
  - Outside the Canary Wharf focus box, outlines under 12 m2 are dropped and rings are simplified at 0.6 m (0.2 m inside).
  - Basements: OSM building:levels:underground, else Wikidata P1139.
  - Floors (focus box only): OSM building:levels (minus building:min_level on a part), else Wikidata P1101.
  - Water level: 25th percentile of the DTM inside the area; areas under 40 m2 (water) or 300 m2 (greens) are dropped.
  - Bridge points more than 20 m above the DTM are taken as covered by a roof and interpolated from the nearest uncovered points on the same way.
  - Tunnel chains for rail, road and foot tunnels; portals at the open level; open cut where the DTM is more than 6 m below the ground 25-35 m around.
  - Riverbed: each UKHO sounding in the box as z_cd minus the chart datum offset interpolated by longitude between 3.20 m and 3.35 m.
  - Flood defence crest: actual_ucl, else actual_dcl, else design_ucl; lines without a crest are dropped.
  - Published levels from data/sourced-levels.json are tunnel controls (nearest point within 150 m); structures with footprint_osm or water_name are drawn from their OSM way or water area.
  - Wikidata places: classes matching the KEEP pattern; classes matching memorial, war memorial, grave or cemetery are left out.
  - Level tags are read by tools/osm-values.mjs osmLevels.
  - Coordinates: WGS84 to BNG through OSTN15; local metres x = E - 537550, z = -(N - 180300) (ORIGIN, One Canada Square to about 20 m); heights in metres above Ordnance Datum Newlyn.
  - Rounding: geometry in whole decimetres, delta-encoded (tools/lib.mjs enc); heights to 0.1 m; control levels to 0.01 m.
  - Fixed faults (F10): simplify() splits a closed ring at its farthest point before Douglas-Peucker; bridge points under a roof are interpolated.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/docklands/lidar/dtm_<E>_<N>.tif` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/docklands/lidar/dsm_<E>_<N>.tif` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/docklands/osm-clip.json.gz` (local, not committed)
  - [data/raw/docklands/wikidata-facts.json.gz](data/raw/docklands/wikidata-facts.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/docklands/wikidata-items.json.gz](data/raw/docklands/wikidata-items.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/docklands/ea-defences.json.gz](data/raw/docklands/ea-defences.json.gz)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_teddington_greenwich_25m.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_teddington_greenwich_25m.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_greenwich_coalhouse_50m.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_greenwich_coalhouse_50m.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_hms_belfast_berth.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_hms_belfast_berth.geojson)
  - [data/sourced-levels.json](data/sourced-levels.json)
  - **pla-tide-booklet** (PLA Tide Booklet 2025, two published numbers used as facts); two chart-datum-to-ODN offsets written into the code: 3.20 m at London Bridge, 3.35 m at North Woolwich
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
  - [docklands/data/under.js](docklands/data/under.js)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: derive; after: `fetch-raw-grid`, `fetch-docklands-lidar`, `osm-clip-docklands`, `fetch-docklands-wikidata`, `fetch-docklands-defences`, `fetch_wet-soundings`
- Hand judgement: The published levels in data/sourced-levels.json are chosen by hand (manual activity sourced-levels); the KEEP class list for Wikidata places is written in the tool.
- Faults: [F10](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F13](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

### 3. Towers

#### 81. `build-towers` (tools/build-towers.py)

- Command: `/opt/towers-venv/bin/python magpie/cwplans/tools/build-towers.py`
- Method: Fits tiered massing (stacked flat tiers, or a pyramid, cone or sloped top where that fits clearly better) to the towers of 100 m or more, from the 1 m EA LiDAR surface model, for the 3D page.
- Rules:
  - Primary survey: the 2022 composite (flights of 2017-12 to 2018-01 and 2020-12-12); the 2018 flight where judgement says so (Novotel, Dollar Bay: under half their roof cells returned in 2020).
  - Dropouts (8 m under the 75th percentile of their 5 x 5 m) filled by a 3 x 3 m grey closing.
  - OSM outline shifted by whole metres (at most 4) onto the LiDAR building.
  - Roof levels: 1-D Gaussian mixture, k by BIC + 2 k ln n; levels closer than 3 m merge.
  - Outlines: open and close, holes under 60 m2 filled, pieces under 16 m2 removed, marching squares, Douglas-Peucker 0.8 m, edges snapped to the dominant direction within 8 degrees.
  - Towers not standing at the 2020 flight keep their OSM outline (status not_in_survey).
  - Hand corrections are listed in the tool (JUDGEMENT) and in each record with its reason.
  - The 2022 composite merges only the 2017-12/2018-01 and 2020-12-12 flights: it is not an independent check of 2020; the independent check is the 2018 flight, for towers finished by January 2018.
  - Composite cells with no return (a gap filled from near the ground) are no data.
  - Roof RMSE is reported on roof cells only.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/dsm/<product>-<year>-<res>-<tile>.zip` (local, not committed)
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
  - [atlas/data/atlas.json](atlas/data/atlas.json)
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/towers.json](docklands/data/towers.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `build-docklands`, `build-atlas`, `fetch-dsm`
- Hand judgement: JUDGEMENT in tools/build-towers.py: the primary survey per tower (2018 for Novotel cwb-0701 and Dollar Bay cwb-0737), levels added by hand (Pan Peninsula cwb-0801: 6.5 and 21.5 m read off the height histogram), a ground level for the height check (8 Canada Square cwb-0417: 9.7 m OD); SKIP lists outlines that are not towers (cwb-0418, cwb-0523). Each reason is in the tool and in the tower record.
- Note: Python with numpy, scipy, scikit-learn, scikit-image, rasterio, shapely, Pillow in /opt/towers-venv. Writes contact sheets to data/raw/towers/ (not committed).

### 4. Water and tides

#### 8. `fetch-docklands-defences` (tools/fetch-docklands.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-docklands.mjs defences`
- Method: Fetches the EA flood defence lines in the Docklands box and keeps only the crest-level and type fields.
- Rules:
  - Kept properties: asset_id, asset_sub_type, protection_type, design_sop, design_ucl, design_dcl, actual_ucl, actual_dcl, effective_cl, bank, local_authority, last_inspection_date.
  - Owner and maintainer fields are dropped (some describe private individuals).
  - Paging stops when a page has no features or no next link.
  - A "fetched" date is written into the file; the pages are fetched in sequence with no pause and no retry; User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1.
- Inputs:
  - **ea-defences** (Environment Agency Spatial Flood Defences, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/spatialdata/spatial-flood-defences-including-standardised-attributes/ogc/features/v1/collections/Spatial_Flood_Defences_Including_Standardised_Attributes/items?bbox=-0.095,51.474,0.015,51.522&limit=500&f=json; OGC API Features items in the Docklands box, following rel=next pages
- Outputs:
  - [data/raw/docklands/ea-defences.json.gz](data/raw/docklands/ea-defences.json.gz)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing

#### 10. `fetch_wet-soundings` (registry/sources/pla/fetch_wet.py)

- Command: `python3 magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/pla/fetch_wet.py soundings`
- Method: Downloads the UKHO bathymetry CSVs and keeps the soundings inside the Docklands box as GeoJSON points with the height above chart datum.
- Rules:
  - Box WGS84 -0.0950, 51.4740 to 0.0150, 51.5220.
  - z_cd is the file value as a height above Admiralty Chart Datum; negative is below chart datum.
  - Lines that do not parse as lat, lon, z (headers) are skipped.
  - Network: curl, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1, 0.6 s pause before every request (at most 2 a second), 180 s timeout, no retry; the CSV is fetched again on every run.
  - Coordinates are rounded to 7 decimals (about 1 cm).
- Inputs:
  - **ukho** (UKHO INSPIRE bathymetry and wrecks, OGL v3.0): https://datahub.admiralty.co.uk/Bathy_Data/Prodbathy/bathymetry/<file>.csv; three survey CSVs by curl: 'River Thames Teddington Lock to Greenwich Reach 25m', 'River Thames Greenwich Reach to Coalhouse Point 50m' (survey RSDRA2018000117951), 'River Thames HMS Belfast' (RSDRA2015000255836)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_teddington_greenwich_25m.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_teddington_greenwich_25m.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_greenwich_coalhouse_50m.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_greenwich_coalhouse_50m.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_hms_belfast_berth.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_hms_belfast_berth.geojson)
  - `data/raw/registry/pla/ukho_<file name with spaces as _>.csv` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Note: Child process: curl. The two ukho_wrecks_obstructions_*.geojson files were made by hand (registry/sources/pla/README.md; manual activity ukho-wrecks-clip), not by this script.

#### 11. `fetch_wet-layers` (registry/sources/pla/fetch_wet.py)

- Command: `python3 magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/pla/fetch_wet.py layers`
- Method: Fetches the PLA and Canal & River Trust layers that intersect the Docklands box and clips them to the box with ogr2ogr.
- Rules:
  - Only the listed outFields are kept per layer ('*' for some).
  - Every layer except pla_hydrographic_product_areas is clipped to the box (ogr2ogr -clipsrc).
  - Arguments 'pla' or 'crt' run one provider only.
  - Network: as fetch_wet-soundings (curl, 0.6 s pause, 180 s timeout); pages of 1000 features follow resultOffset while exceededTransferLimit is true.
  - ogr2ogr writes RFC 7946 GeoJSON with 7 decimals; an older output is removed first.
  - Licence: PLA layers state no licence and Canal & River Trust layers exclude commercial use, so neither is committed (registry/sources/pla/README.md, "Not taken, and why").
- Inputs:
  - **pla-arcgis** (Port of London Authority ArcGIS feature layers, not stated on the layers (see registry/sources/pla/README.md); re-check before production): https://services8.arcgis.com/QgdzYlEcjteGD0VH/arcgis/rest/services/<layer>/FeatureServer/<n>/query; ArcGIS envelope query over the box, outSR 4326, GeoJSON, paged by 1000: 11 Port of London Authority layers (navigational limit, navigation channels, MHWM, hydrographic product areas, foreshore digging and standard permits, restrictions, port masterplan sites, sediment samples, vessel licensing areas, 1968 limits removed by the HRO)
  - **crt-arcgis** (Canal & River Trust ArcGIS feature layers, Canal & River Trust data licence (see registry/sources/pla/README.md)): https://services.arcgis.com/DknzyjEEie5tEW0u/arcgis/rest/services/<layer>/FeatureServer/<n>/query; same query for 10 Canal & River Trust layers (docks, waterway basins, locks, bridges, canals, culverts, mooring sites, towpath, outfalls, stop plank grooves)
- Outputs:
  - `registry/sources/pla/pla_*.geojson` (local, not committed)
  - `registry/sources/pla/crt_*.geojson` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/pla/pla_*.geojson` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/pla/crt_*.geojson` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Note: Child processes: curl, ogr2ogr. The outputs are present in the working tree but not tracked by git (.git/info/exclude covers registry/sources/).

#### 52. `build-river` (tools/build-river.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/build-river.mjs`
- Method: Rasterises the Thames water polygons on an 8 m grid and finds the centreline from the west to the east edge of the model by a least-cost path that keeps away from the banks; also copies the Eden Dock outline.
- Rules:
  - the Thames = tidal water polygons with no name (or named Thames)
  - step cost = step length / (distance to bank + 1)^1.5
  - moving average over 9 cells, resampled every 20 m
  - Start and end: the widest water cell (largest distance to the bank) in the westmost and eastmost grid columns that hold water.
  - Rounding: points and half-widths to 0.1 m; Eden Dock ring to 0.1 m.
- Inputs:
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/river.json](docklands/data/river.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `build-docklands`

#### 66. `fetch-river` (tools/fetch-river.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-river.mjs [pla-notices crt-notices thames-barrier levels osm-river locks eden-dock royal-docks ea-sondes ea-wims river-bus pla-moorings wikidata-vessels] [--no-fetch] [--list]`
- Method: Fetches dated snapshots of the river, docks, locks, water quality, moorings and boats in the Docklands zone (the 3D model box, the Royal Docks margin and a strip north to Bow Locks) and writes one JSON file per source: {meta: source, url, fetched, licence, attribution, method, counts, zone; items: id, kind, time or validity, position {lat, lon, precision, zone}, values, url}.
- Rules:
  - Zone: model box -0.095,51.474 to 0.015,51.522; east margin 0.015,51.495 to 0.085,51.522 (as fetch-works); Lea strip -0.025,51.522 to 0.010,51.528. Server queries use the envelope -0.095,51.474 to 0.085,51.528; items are kept when their point (or one point) falls in the zone.
  - Politeness: one request at a time per host, at least 1.1 s apart (3 s for web.archive.org), up to 5 tries with Retry-After or a doubling pause on 429, 5xx, timeouts and resets; 4xx not retried; User-Agent from tools/lib.mjs. QLever through lib.mjs qlever().
  - PLA (licence not stated) and CRT (no open licence): facts and links only. PLA: the notice number from the link (the name field holds placeholders), kind by title keywords, centroid = mean of polygon vertices, bbox, 'portwide' when the bbox is wider than 0.3 degrees; notice text, polygons, comments, invoice references and staff names dropped. PLA moorings: contact numbers and e-mails dropped.
  - CRT notices: type, reason and programme ids mapped with the lookups embedded in the notices page on 2026-10-04.
  - Thames Barrier: rows of the planned-tests table; London local times converted to ISO with the day's BST/GMT offset.
  - Levels: measures with no readings dropped; series that publish about once a minute (DIFF_*) thinned to the first reading per 15-minute slot plus the latest; no cleaning (Tower Pier low-water fault F21 left for the consumer).
  - Locks: rules hand-written in feeds/river/locks-facts.json; position = centroid of the named OSM element; CRT notices matched by keywords in crt_match against title and waterway; every cited page re-checked by HTTP GET. Conflicting operator statements kept in 'conflicts'.
  - Eden Dock: rows of the table wq-readings-table; temperature from the weather-item-value before 'Water Temperature', stamped with the fetch time. Position: OSM relation 18985240.
  - Royal Docks: pdftotext -layout for per-sample values, -raw for cyanobacteria rows (colonies/ml and the result kept as printed); 'Royal George V Dock' mapped to King George V Dock (written form kept); positions = mean of the vertices in the west or east fifth of the OSM dock polygon, marked approximate.
  - EA sondes: plausibility rules flag (never change) pH outside 6 to 9.5, negative turbidity or oxygen, ammonium over 50 mg/L (fault F24).
  - EA WIMS: BNG to WGS84 by a Helmert transform (proj4, about 5 m); water points get their latest sample from the last 60 observations.
  - TfL: longest stop sequence per line and direction; timetable from the first zone pier; arrivals kept at zone piers only.
  - OSM: osmium extract of the envelope, tags-filter, export geojsonseq; deduplicated by type/id; unnamed seamarks and untyped elements counted, not listed.
  - Raw responses cached under data/raw/river/<source>/ (gitignored); URLs that carry the run's date are cached under a stable key so --no-fetch works; snapshots overwritten on each run (git keeps history).
- Inputs:
  - **pla-ntm-live** (Port of London Authority Notices to Mariners, live ArcGIS layer NtMs_Live, not stated on the item; facts and links only (number, title, dates, reach, closure flag, issuer role, centroid, bbox); notice text, polygons, comments, invoices and staff names not copied): https://maps.pla.co.uk/server/rest/services/Hosted/NtMs_Live/FeatureServer/0/query; where 1=1, envelope -0.095,51.474,0.085,51.528, outFields id, district, name, reach, title, dates, issuer_title, link, closure, archived; geometry WGS84
  - **crt-notices-api** (Canal & River Trust notices and stoppages, © Canal & River Trust, no open licence stated; facts and links only): https://canalrivertrust.org.uk/api/stoppage/notices; consult=false, start = fetch date - 60 days, end = + 240 days, fields title, region, waterways, path, typeId, reasonId, programmeId, start, end, state; geometry=point
  - **govuk-thames-barrier** (GOV.UK guidance 'The Thames Barrier', OGL v3.0): https://www.gov.uk/api/content/guidance/the-thames-barrier; the page body; the planned-tests table and the closure totals
  - **ea-flood-monitoring** (Environment Agency real-time flood-monitoring API: tidal level readings at Tower Pier, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/flood-monitoring/id/stations/{id} and /readings?since=now-48h&_sorted&_limit=10000; /id/floods?lat=51.505&long=0&dist=15; 16 stations: 0007, 0001, 0003, 0006, 4410TH, six DIFF_* barrier stations, 5390TH, 5380TH, 4370TH, 4326TH, 4389TH
  - **crt-pages** (Canal & River Trust boating pages, © Canal & River Trust; lock rules paraphrased by hand as facts with URL and date): two CRT boating pages; HTTP GET to check each page cited in feeds/river/locks-facts.json still answers
  - **southwark-south-dock** (Southwark Council, South Dock Marina navigation page, © Southwark Council; lock facts paraphrased by hand with URL and date): https://www.southwark.gov.uk/culture-and-sport/south-dock-marina/navigation-thames; HTTP GET (page check)
  - **sea-lanes-cw** (Sea Lanes Canary Wharf, all rights reserved; published test results and the shown water temperature kept as facts with URL and fetch date (crawl for scoping, owner 2026-10-03)): https://sealanescanarywharf.co.uk/water-quality/ and /swimming/; HTML GET
  - **rodma-water-quality** (Royal Docks Waterways, all rights reserved; sample results kept as facts with URL and fetch date (crawl for scoping, owner 2026-10-03)): https://royaldockswaterways.com/impact-responsibility/documents/ and each linked 'Water Quality Testing Results' PDF; HTML GET, PDF GET; pdftotext -layout and -raw
  - **ea-hydrology** (Environment Agency Hydrology API: continuous water-quality sondes on the tidal Thames, OGL v3.0 (stated in each response)): https://environment.data.gov.uk/hydrology/id/stations/{id}, /id/measures?station=..., /id/measures/{m}/readings?min-date=..; stations CADOG2, BARIERA, ERITH1; every measure from fetch date - 2 days
  - **ea-water-quality-archive** (Environment Agency Water Quality Archive, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/water-quality/sampling-point and /sampling-point/{id}/observation; Accept: application/ld+json; radius searches (51.498,-0.045,5 km), (51.505,0.045,4.5 km), (51.52,-0.01,2 km), limit 250 with skip; observations limit=1 then the last 60
  - **tfl-unified-api** (Transport for London Unified API: line status by date range, bus route and stop disruptions, road disruptions and closed street segments, TfL open data terms (Transport Data Service; OGL-based, attribution required); the terms page refuses scripted requests: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/Line/Mode/river-bus, /Line/{id}/Route/Sequence/{dir}, /Line/{id}/Timetable/{pier}?direction=, /Line/{ids}/Arrivals; rb1, rb4, rb6, woolwich-ferry; both directions
  - **pla-arcgis** (Port of London Authority ArcGIS feature layers, not stated on the layers (see registry/sources/pla/README.md); re-check before production): https://maps.pla.co.uk/server/rest/services/Visitor_Moorings/FeatureServer and https://services8.arcgis.com/QgdzYlEcjteGD0VH/arcgis/rest/services/Berths_Owners/FeatureServer; first layer, envelope query, geometry WGS84
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://qlever.dev/api/wikidata; one query: P31/P279* watercraft (Q1229765) with P625 in the envelope; label, description, class, P571, P458, P587
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
  - [feeds/river/locks-facts.json](feeds/river/locks-facts.json)
- Outputs:
  - [feeds/river/pla-notices.json](feeds/river/pla-notices.json)
  - [feeds/river/crt-notices.json](feeds/river/crt-notices.json)
  - [feeds/river/thames-barrier.json](feeds/river/thames-barrier.json)
  - [feeds/river/ea-flood-warnings.json](feeds/river/ea-flood-warnings.json)
  - [feeds/river/levels.json](feeds/river/levels.json)
  - [feeds/river/locks.json](feeds/river/locks.json)
  - [feeds/river/eden-dock.json](feeds/river/eden-dock.json)
  - [feeds/river/royal-docks.json](feeds/river/royal-docks.json)
  - [feeds/river/ea-sondes.json](feeds/river/ea-sondes.json)
  - [feeds/river/ea-wims.json](feeds/river/ea-wims.json)
  - [feeds/river/river-bus.json](feeds/river/river-bus.json)
  - [feeds/river/osm-river.json](feeds/river/osm-river.json)
  - [feeds/river/pla-moorings.json](feeds/river/pla-moorings.json)
  - [feeds/river/wikidata-vessels.json](feeds/river/wikidata-vessels.json)
  - `data/raw/river/` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Hand judgement: Licence classes per source (facts only for PLA, CRT and the operators' pages); the lock rules in feeds/river/locks-facts.json are read and paraphrased by hand (recorded with URL and date); the sonde plausibility thresholds; the choice of stations and sondes. Recorded in skills/cwplans-river-and-water/SKILL.md and feeds/river/README.md.
- Faults: [F21](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F24](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 68. `fetch-ais` (tools/fetch-ais.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-ais.mjs [--listen=600] [--no-fetch]`
- Method: Reads a GeoJSON snapshot of vessels in the zone envelope, the station list and the SSE stream for a bounded time; classes each event by its own licence; writes kept vessels as dated facts and counts everything else.
- Rules:
  - Keep an event only when license is CC0-1.0, NLOD-2.0 or CC-BY-4.0; snapshot features by the source of the last message (station:/udp:/mmsi: = CC0, kystverket/barentswatch = NLOD, digitraffic = CC BY).
  - Fields of a kept vessel only from kept events or kept snapshot features.
  - Small private craft (ITU 36/37; class B without a commercial type 30-35 or 40-99; class and type not heard) counted, never listed.
  - AIS sentinels dropped (COG 360, SOG 102.3, heading 511, nav status 15); positions rounded to 5 decimals.
  - Not kept: counted by type group in meta.not_kept_would_gain, no ids or names.
  - Politeness: one HTTP request at a time >= 2 s apart, one stream, User-Agent from tools/lib.mjs; raw runs gitignored.
- Inputs:
  - **openwaters-ais** (Open Waters AIS, Per event, the licence of the upstream source (the aggregate is not relicensed). Only events from open sources are kept (see openwaters-volunteer-cc0); AISHub and aisstream.io events are not kept (owner decision). Service: free tiers 'for personal use'; commercial use of the hosted service is a paid tier): https://ais.openwaters.io/v1/vessels, /v1/stations, /v1/stream (SSE); bbox=51.474,-0.095,51.528,0.085 (minLat,minLon,maxLat,maxLon); stream with snapshot=1 for --listen seconds (default 600, max 1800); anonymous tier, no key
- Outputs:
  - [feeds/river/ais.json](feeds/river/ais.json)
  - `data/raw/ais/` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Hand judgement: Owner decision 2026-10-04: AISHub and aisstream.io events are kept for scoping (licence class scoping-accepted-2026-10-04), marked for review before scaling or commercial use. Before that decision: Open Waters re-serves each event under its upstream source's terms. Only open licences were kept (CC0 single volunteer receptions, NLOD, CC BY). AISHub (a private written assurance reported by Open Waters, revocable) and aisstream.io (no terms) are not kept without the owner's agreement; on 2026-10-04 they were the whole vessel coverage of the zone, so 0 vessels were kept and 3 CC0 aids to navigation.

#### Hand step `ukho-wrecks-clip`: UKHO wrecks and obstructions clipped to the box

- By: hand; date: 2026-10-02
- Method: Clips the UKHO INSPIRE wrecks and obstructions shapefile to the Docklands box with ogr2ogr (the command is in registry/sources/pla/README.md).
- Rules:
  - Box WGS84 -0.0950, 51.4740 to 0.0150, 51.5220.
  - Depths (valsou) are metres below chart datum; not for navigation.
- Inputs:
  - **ukho** (UKHO INSPIRE bathymetry and wrecks, OGL v3.0)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_wrecks_obstructions_points.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_wrecks_obstructions_points.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_wrecks_obstructions_areas.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_wrecks_obstructions_areas.geojson)
- Decisions recorded in: registry/sources/pla/README.md

### 5. Underground and levels

#### 38. `build-indoor` (tools/build-indoor.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/build-indoor.mjs`
- Method: Builds the Canary Wharf walking network in 3D from OSM (footways, corridors, stairs, escalators, lifts, platforms, streets) with each point on its level, adds TfL's step-free station topology, attaches named places, and records faults for the audit.
- Rules:
  - Box -0.0330, 51.4960 to -0.0020, 51.5120; walkable highway values, not access=private or foot=no, plus platforms, conveying ways and indoor areas, corridors and rooms.
  - Level: level, else level:ref (tools/osm-values.mjs osmLevels), else layer when the way is a tunnel, indoor or underground with layer < 0, else 0.
  - One vertex per (OSM node, level); stairs and escalators are oriented by the levels their ends touch, else by incline, else counted as unresolved.
  - A lift node joins its copies at the levels it lists, in order.
  - Walkable areas get a hub joined to every vertex on their level inside them or within 3 m of their edge.
  - A node that ordinary ways reach at two levels with no connector is joined as 'ramp' and counted as a level jump.
  - TfL points join the nearest OSM vertex within 35 m only at street level (TfL level 0 = OSM level 0); TfL lifts (pathway_mode 5) and paths join TfL points; nearest-vertex level pairs are recorded per station.
  - Places attach to the nearest vertex on their own level within 40 m, else the nearest vertex on any level within 40 m (marked).
  - Cache: the TfL GTFS ZIP is kept in data/raw/docklands/ and fetched only when absent (one GET, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1).
  - Coordinates: OSTN15 to BNG, local metres as build-docklands; positions rounded to 0.1 m.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
  - [data/raw/docklands/tfl-stationdata-gtfs.zip](data/raw/docklands/tfl-stationdata-gtfs.zip)
  - **tfl-open-data** (Transport for London open data: step-free station topology, TfL open data terms (Powered by TfL Open Data; OGL-based); the terms page was not readable from the container: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/stationdata/tfl-stationdata-gtfs.zip; TfL station topology (GTFS stops and pathways), fetched only when the local copy is absent
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/indoor.js](docklands/data/indoor.js)
  - [data/raw/docklands/tfl-stationdata-gtfs.zip](data/raw/docklands/tfl-stationdata-gtfs.zip)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: derive; after: `fetch-docklands-osm`, `build-docklands`, `fetch-raw-grid`
- Faults: [F2](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F12](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)
- Note: network is true only when the GTFS ZIP is absent. Child process: unzip -p.

#### Hand step `sourced-levels`: Published levels used as tunnel and structure controls

- By: hand (main session and research agents); date: 2026-10-02/03
- Method: Picks, from the cited facts in docklands/facts.json and from TfL freedom-of-information rail levels, the station, tunnel and structure levels that the 3D builds apply as controls, each with its datum, source URL and short quote.
- Rules:
  - A level states its datum: od (metres above Ordnance Datum Newlyn) or below_ground (metres below the LiDAR ground at the point).
  - The asset owner's level (TfL rail level; London Underground Datum = OD - 100 m) outranks a rounded secondary depth; the loser stays in the file with superseded_by and is not applied (F13).
  - Each control names the line pattern and kinds of tunnel chain it applies to; structures name footprint_osm or water_name to be drawn from OSM.
  - Only short quotes are kept; plans and drawings are for reference by eye (feeds/underground/README.md).
- Inputs:
  - [docklands/facts.json](docklands/facts.json)
  - **tfl-foi** (TfL FOI-0493-2223, FOI disclosure; no open licence stated. Only six cited values are used, as facts with their source row; re-check before production)
  - **web-quotes** (short quotations from published pages, Wikipedia text is CC BY-SA 4.0 (share-alike); the other pages are all rights reserved)
- Outputs:
  - [data/sourced-levels.json](data/sourced-levels.json)
- Decisions recorded in: data/sourced-levels.json (source, quote, superseded_by per control)
- Faults: [F13](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### Hand step `docklands-facts`: Cited facts on the underground and multi-storey structure

- By: research agents, checked by hand; date: 2026-10-02
- Method: Research agents collected 360 numeric facts (depths, tunnels, towers, docks, ground) with a source URL and a short quote each, and 19 recorded gaps; a script checked every quote against the cached copy of the page it cites (whitespace normalised); Wikidata values come from SPARQL with the statement as the quote. FACTS.md is the same facts as tables.
- Rules:
  - Each fact keeps value, unit, datum, source URL and quote.
  - Datums are named: OD, CD (3.35 m below OD at West India Docks), mATD (OD + 100 m).
  - Gaps are recorded, not filled by guesses.
- Inputs:
  - **web-quotes** (short quotations from published pages, Wikipedia text is CC BY-SA 4.0 (share-alike); the other pages are all rights reserved)
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/facts.json](docklands/facts.json)
  - [docklands/FACTS.md](docklands/FACTS.md)
- Decisions recorded in: docklands/facts.json; docklands/FACTS.md
- Not committed as code: The quote-check script and the FACTS.md generator were one-off scripts and are not committed: the method is recorded here, the code is not.

#### Hand step `underground-catalogue`: Source catalogue for the underground estate

- By: four research agents; date: 2026-10-03
- Method: Four research agents searched for plans, levels and 3D models of the malls, stations and tunnels; each entry was fetched directly or as an Internet Archive copy and its licence read; sources not used are listed with the reason. The README tables are generated from the JSON files.
- Rules:
  - Nothing is copied into the model except cited level values (data/sourced-levels.json).
  - Copyright plans (Canary Wharf Group, TfL, Crossrail) are for reference by eye, not for copying.
  - Each exclusion records why (licence, no levels, duplicate, unreachable).
- Inputs:
  - **web-quotes** (short quotations from published pages, Wikipedia text is CC BY-SA 4.0 (share-alike); the other pages are all rights reserved)
- Outputs:
  - [feeds/underground/stations.json](feeds/underground/stations.json)
  - [feeds/underground/planning.json](feeds/underground/planning.json)
  - [feeds/underground/estate.json](feeds/underground/estate.json)
  - [feeds/underground/tunnels.json](feeds/underground/tunnels.json)
  - [feeds/underground/README.md](feeds/underground/README.md)
- Decisions recorded in: feeds/underground/*-excluded.json
- Not committed as code: The generator of feeds/underground/README.md is not committed.

### 6. Postcodes

#### 14. `postcodes-fetch` (tools/postcodes.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/postcodes.mjs fetch`
- Method: Fetches every ONSPD row in district E14 and the ward names and Canary Wharf ward boundary, then tallies the OSM features that carry each E14 postcode.
- Rules:
  - Row batches are retried up to 4 times.
  - OSM: an addr:postcode ';' list counts the feature under each postcode (fault F2); kinds counted: shop, amenity:<value>, office, building, entrance, other; streets from addr:street.
  - Network: User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; queries in sequence; a batch of 200 ids is tried up to 4 times with 4 s x try between tries; the ward queries are not retried. Files are overwritten on every run.
- Inputs:
  - **onspd** (ONS Postcode Directory, August 2026, OGL v3.0): https://services1.arcgis.com/ESMARspQHYMw9BZ9/arcgis/rest/services/ONS_Postcode_Directory_(August_2026)_(Hosted_Table)/FeatureServer/0/query; object ids where pcds LIKE 'E14 %', then rows by 200 ids with pcds, dointr, doterm, usrtypind, east1m, north1m, gridind, lat, long, wd26cd, lad26cd, oa21cd, lsoa21cd
  - **ons-boundaries** (ONS ward names and boundaries, OGL v3.0): https://services1.arcgis.com/ESMARspQHYMw9BZ9/arcgis/rest/services/WD_MAY_2026_UK_BFE/FeatureServer/0/query; WD26CD, WD26NM, LAD26NM of every ward the rows name; the geometry of ward E05009323 (Canary Wharf) as GeoJSON in EPSG:4326
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [data/raw/postcodes/onspd-aug2026.json.gz](data/raw/postcodes/onspd-aug2026.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/postcodes/wards.json](data/raw/postcodes/wards.json)
  - [data/raw/postcodes/osm-addr-postcodes.json](data/raw/postcodes/osm-addr-postcodes.json)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `fetch-docklands-osm`
- Faults: [F2](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)
- Note: The ward GeoJSON is stored inside wards.json; postcodes/canary-wharf-ward.geojson is written by the build mode (postcodes-build).

#### 21. `postcodes-osm` (tools/postcodes.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/postcodes.mjs osm`
- Method: Only the OSM address tally of the fetch mode, without network: features, streets and kinds per E14 postcode.
- Rules:
  - An addr:postcode ';' list counts the feature under each postcode (fault F2).
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [data/raw/postcodes/osm-addr-postcodes.json](data/raw/postcodes/osm-addr-postcodes.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `fetch-docklands-osm`
- Faults: [F2](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 24. `postcodes-build` (tools/postcodes.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/postcodes.mjs build`
- Method: Lists every syntactically possible unit postcode in the E14 sectors ONSPD knows, marks each as never allocated, live or terminated, joins ward, Canary Wharf box and OSM address counts, and gives each a tier.
- Rules:
  - Possible units: every sector in the rows times two letters from ABDEFGHJLNPQRSTUWXYZ.
  - Live: no termination date (doterm).
  - In the Canary Wharf ward: wd26cd is E05009323. In the box: ONSPD lat/long inside -0.0300, 51.4980 to -0.0050, 51.5100.
  - Tiers: possible-unallocated; terminated-cw or terminated-other; cw-core (box and ward), cw-ward, cw-box-other-ward, e14-other.
  - large_user: usrtypind = 1. Distance to One Canada Square from east1m/north1m to E 537550, N 180300.
  - The CSV leaves out the never-allocated codes.
- Inputs:
  - [data/raw/postcodes/onspd-aug2026.json.gz](data/raw/postcodes/onspd-aug2026.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/postcodes/wards.json](data/raw/postcodes/wards.json)
  - [data/raw/postcodes/osm-addr-postcodes.json](data/raw/postcodes/osm-addr-postcodes.json)
- Outputs:
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
  - [postcodes/postcodes.csv](postcodes/postcodes.csv)
  - [postcodes/canary-wharf-ward.geojson](postcodes/canary-wharf-ward.geojson)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: join; after: `postcodes-fetch`, `postcodes-osm`
- Faults: [F2](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 25. `postcodes-query` (tools/postcodes.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/postcodes.mjs query [tier ...]`
- Method: Runs postcode-keyed searches for the Canary Wharf postcodes against GOV.UK, Wikipedia, the FSA API and Wikidata, and stores hit counts, titles and links, with search-engine templates for manual use.
- Rules:
  - Default tiers: cw-core, cw-ward, cw-box-other-ward.
  - Per host one request about every second; each request is tried 3 times.
  - FSA results keep only premises whose postcode is the one asked and that have an address line (home businesses are skipped).
  - Network: one worker per host (GOV.UK, Wikipedia, FSA), in parallel across hosts; 1 s pause after every postcode; 3 tries with 3 s x try between; Wikidata by query.wikidata.org SPARQL, 200 postcodes a query. User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1. No cache: queries.json is rewritten.
- Inputs:
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
  - **govuk-search** (GOV.UK search API, OGL v3.0): https://www.gov.uk/api/search.json; "<postcode>" in 16 allowed document formats (news, notices, consultations, guidance, accident reports, CMA cases, statistics and others), 10 results: title, link, date, format
  - **wikipedia-search** (Wikipedia search API, article text CC BY-SA (not stored); titles and URLs only): https://en.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?action=query&list=search; full-text search for "<postcode>", 10 results: titles and URLs
  - **fsa-api** (Food Standards Agency food hygiene rating API, OGL v3.0): https://api.ratings.food.gov.uk/Establishments?address=<postcode>&pageSize=200; FSA ratings API, premises at the postcode
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://query.wikidata.org/sparql; SPARQL: items whose P281 postal code is one of the postcodes (200 a query)
- Outputs:
  - [postcodes/queries.json](postcodes/queries.json)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `postcodes-build`

### 7. Registry and occupants

#### 15. `registry-wikidata` (tools/registry-wikidata.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/registry-wikidata.mjs`
- Method: Collects the Wikidata items around Canary Wharf with the properties the registry uses, and the organisations headquartered in them.
- Rules:
  - People (Q5) are excluded as items and as property values, except architect (P84) and structural engineer (P631).
  - Items whose P31 label matches memorial, grave, cemetery, tomb or plaque are dropped.
  - Duplicate property values are removed.
  - Network: QLever through tools/lib.mjs qlever(): one query at a time, at least 1.5 s apart, up to 5 tries with backoff on 429 and 5xx (owner rule, 2026-10-03); User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; no cache.
  - Distance km rounded to 3 decimals; each item keeps its QID (an IRI key for the knowledge graph).
- Inputs:
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://qlever.dev/api/wikidata; SPARQL: items with P625 within 1.1 km of POINT(-0.0175 51.5040) that are not Q5, with English label and description
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://qlever.dev/api/wikidata; SPARQL by VALUES (150 items a query): P31, P127, P466, P1101, P1139, P2048, P571, P576, P1619, P281, P6375, P669, P670, P856, P84, P631, P1435, P159, P452, P749 with English value labels
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://qlever.dev/api/wikidata; SPARQL: organisations (not Q5, no P576) whose P159 headquarters is an item in the radius, with P856 website
- Outputs:
  - [data/raw/registry/wikidata-cw.json](data/raw/registry/wikidata-cw.json)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Faults: [F8](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 16. `registry-fhrs` (tools/registry-fhrs.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/registry-fhrs.mjs`
- Method: Saves a dated snapshot of the E14 premises in the FSA Tower Hamlets file and, when an earlier snapshot exists, lists the premises added and removed between the last two.
- Rules:
  - Kept: postcode starting E14 and at least one address line.
  - Premises without address lines (home businesses whose address the FSA withholds) are not kept.
  - Network: one GET of the whole file, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; no retry. Each run writes a new dated snapshot; the snapshot date is the run date.
- Inputs:
  - **fsa** (FSA Food Hygiene Rating data, Tower Hamlets, OGL v3.0): https://ratings.food.gov.uk/api/open-data-files/FHRS530en-GB.json; the whole Tower Hamlets open-data file
  - [data/raw/registry/fhrs/FHRS530-2026-10-03.json.gz](data/raw/registry/fhrs/FHRS530-2026-10-03.json.gz)
- Outputs:
  - [data/raw/registry/fhrs/FHRS530-2026-10-03.json.gz](data/raw/registry/fhrs/FHRS530-2026-10-03.json.gz)
  - `registry/fhrs-changes.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Faults: [F7](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 18. `registry-osm` (tools/registry-osm.mjs)

- Command: `node --max-old-space-size=6000 magpie/cwplans/tools/registry-osm.mjs`
- Method: Extracts every tagged OSM node, every way with a node in the Canary Wharf box, and the building or named multipolygon relations that use those ways, with full tags.
- Rules:
  - Box -0.0300, 51.4980 to -0.0050, 51.5100 plus a 0.0015 degree margin.
  - Tags dropped: contact:mobile, mobile, operator:person, addr:flats, note, fixme, FIXME, source*, created_by. Business contact tags (phone, contact:phone, email, contact:email, website, contact:*) are kept from 2026-10-03 (owner: cwplans exception, organisation data allowed in the prototyping phase).
  - Relations kept: type multipolygon or building with building, building:part or name, and at least one kept member way.
  - Tags are kept as strings; ";" lists are split later by tools/osm-values.mjs osmList (F2) in the tools that read them.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz](data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: clip; after: `fetch-docklands-osm`
- Faults: [F2](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F15](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)
- Note: Also imported as a module by build-registry.mjs (CW_BOX).

#### 26. `registry-uprn` (tools/registry-uprn.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/registry-uprn.mjs`
- Method: Keeps the UPRNs whose point is in the Canary Wharf box, with their TOID and USRN links, the nearest live postcode centroid, and summary counts.
- Rules:
  - Kept: UPRN latitude and longitude inside the Canary Wharf box; the Docklands box is only counted.
  - nearest_postcode_centroid: the nearest live ONSPD centroid by BNG distance (an approximation; Open UPRN has no postcode).
  - LIDS rows are kept when their UPRN is one of the kept UPRNs.
- Inputs:
  - **os-open-uprn** (OS Open UPRN and Open Linked Identifiers, OGL v3.0): https://api.os.uk/downloads/v1/products/OpenUPRN/downloads and https://api.os.uk/downloads/v1/products/LIDS/downloads; national OS Open UPRN CSV and the two LIDS link files [manual download]
  - `data/raw/registry/osopenuprn_202609_csv.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/lids-2026-09_csv_BLPU-UPRN-TopographicArea-TOID-5.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/lids-2026-09_csv_BLPU-UPRN-Street-USRN-11.zip` (local, not committed)
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
- Outputs:
  - `registry/sources/uprn/uprn-canary-wharf.csv` (local, not committed)
  - `registry/sources/uprn/uprn-linked-ids-canary-wharf.csv` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/sources/uprn/uprn-summary.json](registry/sources/uprn/uprn-summary.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: clip; after: `postcodes-build`
- Note: Child process: unzip -p.

#### 27. `registry-lbsm` (tools/registry-lbsm.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/registry-lbsm.mjs`
- Method: Aggregates the LBSM2 homes in the Canary Wharf postcodes or box per postcode and per MasterMap TOID: homes, property type, built form, age band, EPC band, floor area median, maximum floor count.
- Rules:
  - A home is kept when its postcode_locator is a Canary Wharf postcode (cwPostcodes, terminated included) or its UPRN is in uprn-canary-wharf.csv.
  - Only the fields property_type, built_form, construction_age_band(_known), epc_rating(_known), total_floor_area, estimated_floor_count, building_use are read; household fields (tenure, fuel poverty, deprivation) are never read out.
  - No per-home rows are written.
- Inputs:
  - **lbsm** (GLA London Building Stock Model 2, OGL v3.0): https://data.london.gov.uk/dataset/2k55d; LBSM2 Tower Hamlets CSV (one row per home) [manual download]
  - `data/raw/registry/lbsm/LBSMv2_Tower_Hamlets.csv` (local, not committed)
  - `registry/sources/uprn/uprn-canary-wharf.csv` (local, not committed)
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
- Outputs:
  - `registry/sources/uprn/lbsm2-homes-summary.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: derive; after: `postcodes-build`, `registry-uprn`

#### 28. `registry-inspire` (tools/registry-inspire.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/registry-inspire.mjs`
- Method: Keeps the INSPIRE freehold index polygons that intersect the Canary Wharf box, converted from BNG to WGS84 through OSTN15, with area and centroid computed in BNG.
- Rules:
  - Prefilter: a polygon with a vertex in the box's BNG bounds plus 50 m, or whose bounding box overlaps them.
  - Kept when an exterior vertex is in the box, a box corner is inside the exterior, or an exterior edge crosses the box.
  - Area: exterior minus holes, summed over the polygons of a member.
- Inputs:
  - **hmlr-inspire** (HM Land Registry INSPIRE Index Polygons, OGL v3.0 with conditions): https://use-land-property-data.service.gov.uk/datasets/inspire/download/London_Borough_of_Tower_Hamlets.zip; the Tower Hamlets INSPIRE Index Polygons GML [manual download]
  - `data/raw/registry/inspire/Land_Registry_Cadastral_Parcels.gml` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/landregistry/inspire-canary-wharf.geojson](registry/sources/landregistry/inspire-canary-wharf.geojson)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: clip; after: `fetch-raw-grid`

#### 29. `registry-companies` (tools/registry-companies.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/registry-companies.mjs [path/to/BasicCompanyDataAsOneFile-YYYY-MM-DD.zip]`
- Method: Streams the national company file and keeps the companies whose registered-office postcode is a Canary Wharf postcode, with company-level fields only, plus a summary by postcode, status, category, SIC and year.
- Rules:
  - Kept: RegAddress.PostCode, normalised, in cwPostcodes() (tiers cw-core, cw-ward, cw-box-other-ward, terminated-cw).
  - A 'care of' clause in an address line that does not look like an organisation (ORG word list) is replaced by '[withheld]'; a CareOf field is kept only when it looks like an organisation.
  - No officers or persons with significant control are read (the product has none).
- Inputs:
  - **companies-house** (Companies House Basic Company Data, free reuse (OGL terms for Companies House public data)): https://download.companieshouse.gov.uk/en_output.html; Basic Company Data, one-file CSV in a ZIP (about 0.5 GB) [manual download]
  - `data/raw/registry/BasicCompanyDataAsOneFile-2026-10-01.zip` (local, not committed)
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
- Outputs:
  - `registry/sources/companies/companies-by-postcode.json` (local, not committed)
  - `registry/sources/companies/companies-summary.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: parse; after: `postcodes-build`
- Note: Child process: unzip -p.

#### 30. `registry-landregistry` (tools/registry-landregistry.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/registry-landregistry.mjs`
- Method: Fetches every Price Paid transaction for the Canary Wharf postcodes and writes them per postcode with a summary of homes, sales, years, types and median prices.
- Rules:
  - Postcodes: cwPostcodes() (terminated-cw included).
  - Rows repeated by the OPTIONAL joins are removed by transaction id.
  - Homes = distinct SAON + PAON + street of types other than 'other'.
  - Network: User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; 0.7 s pause after each query of 20 postcodes; a failed query is tried up to 4 times with 3, 6, 9 s between tries; no cache.
- Inputs:
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
  - **hmlr-price-paid** (HM Land Registry Price Paid Data, OGL v3.0): https://landregistry.data.gov.uk/landregistry/query; SPARQL, 20 postcodes a query: transactions at addresses with those postcodes: price, date, property type, new build, estate type, transaction category, PAON, SAON, street, town
- Outputs:
  - `registry/sources/landregistry/price-paid-by-postcode.json` (local, not committed)
  - `registry/sources/landregistry/price-paid-summary.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `postcodes-build`

#### 31. `registry-voa` (tools/registry-voa.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/registry-voa.mjs [path/to/list-entries.zip]`
- Method: Filters the 2026 rating list to the Canary Wharf postcodes: a per-property extract kept locally, and per-postcode aggregates of entries, rateable value, streets and descriptions.
- Rules:
  - Kept: list entries whose postcode (field 14) is in cwPostcodes().
  - The firm's-name field is never read.
- Inputs:
  - **voa-rating-list** (Valuation Office Agency non-domestic rating list 2026, VOA licence with restrictions: never committed (CLAUDE.md licence limit); local use only): https://voaratinglists.blob.core.windows.net/html/rlidata.htm; VOA non-domestic rating list 2026, list entries ZIP [manual download]
  - `data/raw/registry/voa/ndr-2026-listentries-*.zip` (local, not committed)
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/registry/voa/voa-ndr-2026-canary-wharf.json` (local, not committed)
  - `registry/sources/companies/voa-ndr-2026-summary.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: parse; after: `postcodes-build`
- Hand judgement: Licence decision: the VOA rating list is restricted, so neither output is committed (owner to decide).
- Note: Child process: unzip -p. Both outputs are uncommitted, as the licence requires (CLAUDE.md names the VOA list as restricted).

#### 73. `build-registry` (tools/build-registry.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/build-registry.mjs`
- Method: Builds the Canary Wharf building registry: one record per OSM building outline with a stable cwb- id, its parts, occupants (OSM, FSA, Wikidata, chain-store branches, the Canary Wharf Group directory, the regulatory and public registers), owners, postcodes, Wikidata facts, and company and property joins.
- Rules:
  - A building is an OSM building outline (way or multipolygon) whose vertex mean is in the Canary Wharf box; building:part shapes are listed under the outline that contains their centre.
  - Ids: reused from registry/ids.json by OSM element; new outlines get the next number in reading order (north to south in 100 m bands, then west to east); a retired id is never reused.
  - OSM occupants: named features with a shop, food and drink, office, amenity, leisure, tourism, healthcare, craft or transport role, placed in the outline that contains them or the nearest outline within 12 m; artworks, information boards, viewpoints and attractions are features, not occupants. Each OSM occupant keeps its tags opening_hours, cuisine, wheelchair, check_date, start_date, opening_date, operator, url, level, brand, brand:wikidata, website (or contact:website), phone (or contact:phone), email (or contact:email) and the other contact:* links (as contact).
  - FSA premises with a position in the box are placed the same way; the others are listed under their postcode when its tier is cw-*.
  - Wikidata: an OSM wikidata tag wins; else the one building-like item whose coordinate is in the outline (within 5 m) and no other building-like item is inside it; an item already named by an OSM tag is not attached to a second outline; other items inside are occupants 'located here'.
  - The Wikidata label replaces the OSM name; the OSM name is kept as osm_name.
  - Owners: Wikidata P127. Occupants also from P466 and from organisations whose P159 is the building.
  - UPRN points inside the outline (no tolerance) give UPRN counts, TOIDs and USRNs; LBSM2 homes are summed over the building's TOIDs.
  - Home figures are given only where at least 5 homes are counted (K = 5); otherwise 'fewer than 5'.
  - INSPIRE polygons that contain the building's centre are linked by INSPIRE id.
  - Companies and price-paid sales join a building when the postcode is one of its postcodes and the normalised address contains one of its keys (address, name, OSM name, Wikidata street address), and exactly one building matches.
  - A residential building lists only the count of registered companies, not their names.
  - Branches enrich the occupant with the same OSM or FSA id, else are placed by position (marked approximate when the position is a mall or postcode centre).
  - Buildings keep the OSM addr:housename (housename): it names the complex for a mall outline (Canada Place on 16-19 Canada Square).
  - CWG directory: an entry already linked by the branch join keeps that link. Else it joins existing occupants (OSM, FSA, Wikidata, branches, and CWG occupants added earlier in the same pass) when a name key is equal (registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-fields.mjs nameKeys) and the place agrees, tried in this order: same mall (or same street or square), a shared postcode, or the building the entry itself would be placed in. Two different named malls never join (Canada Place and the One Canada Square mall count as one). An occupant with a CWG link takes a second entry only from another CWG section (cwg_also). Records of one source in two buildings: ambiguous, not joined.
