  - CWG entries that join nothing become occupants (source cwg, role directory: <section>) placed by: the one building whose address key equals the street line (confidence high with the postcode, medium without); else the host outline of a named mall (the one outline whose OSM name, else addr:housename, is the mall name or starts with it; medium); else the one registry building with the entry postcode, or the one of them OSM tags as retail (low). Estate-wide entries, car parks and entries with no page, address or single building stay unplaced with a reason (cwg_unplaced, summary.joins.cwg).
  - Registers (registry/sources/registers/): records are excluded when the address is not a place: GIAS correspondence addresses (address_role), the registered-office service at E14 5HU / 10th floor 5 Churchill Place, care-of and accountant addresses. Keys in order: UPRN (OS Open UPRN point inside an outline; not used when one register gives the UPRN to records at two or more postcodes, or when the UPRN point is over 150 m from the register point), the register point (inside or within 12 m; an FSA point within 3 m of its postcode centre is a postcode, F7), a named mall in the address (the mall host outline, F4; the point outline kept as point_in), street address with the postcode, street address alone when one building matches a numbered address key, the name of an occupant at the same postcode or mall, the postcode alone when one registry building has it, the ONSPD centre is within 50 m of it and the address names no other street (never for a charity). A charity placed by address in a residential building (homes or a residential building tag) is left unplaced. A record whose name matches an occupant of the chosen building enriches it; else it is a new occupant (source = register, role register: <name> or head office (ODS) for ODS organisation records, current false for closed or inactive records). Each link (occupant.registers[]) keeps register, id, kind, status, key, precision, confidence; source ids are copied to the occupant (urn, ods_code, cqc_location_id, charity_number, company_number, ofsted_urn, gambling_premises_id, active_places_site_id, fhrs_id). Unplaced records and rejected UPRNs are listed (registers_unplaced, registers_uprn_rejected).
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
  - [data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz](data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/registry/wikidata-cw.json](data/raw/registry/wikidata-cw.json)
  - [data/raw/registry/fhrs/FHRS530-2026-10-03.json.gz](data/raw/registry/fhrs/FHRS530-2026-10-03.json.gz)
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
  - [registry/ids.json](registry/ids.json)
  - `registry/sources/uprn/uprn-canary-wharf.csv` (local, not committed)
  - `registry/sources/uprn/uprn-linked-ids-canary-wharf.csv` (local, not committed)
  - `registry/sources/uprn/lbsm2-homes-summary.json` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/sources/landregistry/inspire-canary-wharf.geojson](registry/sources/landregistry/inspire-canary-wharf.geojson)
  - `registry/sources/companies/companies-by-postcode.json` (local, not committed)
  - `registry/sources/landregistry/price-paid-by-postcode.json` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/gias.json](registry/sources/registers/gias.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/cqc.json](registry/sources/registers/cqc.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/ods.json](registry/sources/registers/ods.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/charities.json](registry/sources/registers/charities.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/ofsted-childcare.json](registry/sources/registers/ofsted-childcare.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/gambling.json](registry/sources/registers/gambling.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/active-places.json](registry/sources/registers/active-places.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/fsa-pubs.json](registry/sources/registers/fsa-pubs.json)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/ids.json](registry/ids.json)
  - [registry/companies-by-postcode.json](registry/companies-by-postcode.json)
  - [registry/homes-by-postcode.json](registry/homes-by-postcode.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: join; after: `fetch-raw-grid`, `registry-osm`, `registry-wikidata`, `registry-fhrs`, `postcodes-build`, `registry-uprn`, `registry-lbsm`, `registry-inspire`, `registry-companies`, `registry-landregistry`, `build-branches`, `fetch-cwg`, `fetch-registers`
- Faults: [F1](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F2](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F4](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F6](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F7](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F8](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F9](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F15](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F16](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F17](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F18](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F19](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F20](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 78. `build-categories` (tools/build-categories.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/build-categories.mjs`
- Method: Gives each registry building its occupant categories (finance, shop, catering, leisure, entertainment; and bar, alcohol, education, health, sport, arts, charity) from the classes the sources state, for the glow outlines and the atlas.
- Rules:
  - finance: OSM amenity bank, bureau_de_change or money_transfer, or office financial, insurance, financial_advisor or tax_advisor; or a Wikidata P31 or P452 label matching bank, finance, insurance, investment and similar; or the role 'service: bank' or 'branch: bank'.
  - shop: an OSM shop tag, the role 'shop' or 'branch: shop', or an FSA type starting 'Retailers'.
  - catering: OSM amenity restaurant, cafe, bar, pub, fast_food, food_court, ice_cream or biergarten; the role 'food and drink' or a food branch; or an FSA catering type.
  - leisure: OSM leisure fitness_centre, sports_centre, swimming_pool, sauna, dance or sports_hall; a hotel role or FSA hotel type.
  - entertainment: OSM cinema, theatre, nightclub, arts centre, casino, events or music venue, planetarium, amusement and bowling leisure, museum or gallery; or a Wikidata class matching those.
  - bar: OSM amenity pub, bar, nightclub or biergarten; FSA type Pub/bar/nightclub; CWG category Bars or Cocktails; a Wikidata class pub, bar, nightclub.
  - alcohol: OSM shop alcohol, wine or beverages.
  - education: OSM amenity school, college, university, kindergarten or childcare; FSA type School/college/university; CWG category Childcare; a Wikidata class school, university, college, academic institution, kindergarten or nursery; the role branch: childcare.
  - health: OSM amenity doctors, dentist, pharmacy, clinic or hospital, or any healthcare tag; CWG category Healthcare unless OSM says amenity=veterinary; a Wikidata class hospital, clinic, doctor's office, medical organization, dental, pharmacy.
  - sport: OSM leisure sports_centre, fitness_centre, pitch, swimming_pool or sports_hall, or any sport tag; CWG category Sport (not Sport & Fitness, which lists sportswear shops); a Wikidata sports venue or gym class; the role branch: gym.
  - arts: OSM amenity cinema, theatre, arts_centre, nightclub, music_venue, events_venue or concert_hall, or any theatre:genre tag; a Wikidata class movie theater, cinema, theatre, music venue, concert hall, arts centre or nightclub.
  - CWG directory classes for the first five: section restaurant gives catering, section stay or category Hotels & Apartments gives leisure, category Banks & Foreign Exchange gives finance, and goods categories (Shoes & Footwear, Menswear, Jewellery & Watches and others listed in the tool) give shop. CWG labels that mix classes (Services, Health & Beauty, Cafes & Bars, Sport & Fitness) and the FSA type Hospitals/Childcare/Caring Premises are not used.
  - Registers (current links only): GIAS, Ofsted childcare and ODS School give education; CQC health services (doctors, dentists, diagnosis, urgent care, clinics, hospitals, community healthcare, nursing homes) and ODS service sites (pharmacies, surgeries, GP practices, dental practices, optical sites, NHS trust, independent provider, PCT and care trust sites) give health; Active Places gives sport; FSA pubs give bar; gambling premises (betting shops) give shop; the Charity Commission gives charity. ODS organisation records, ODS Education (NHS training bodies) and social care services give no category. An occupant with current false counts as former.
  - Names are never used to guess a class; Companies House registered offices are not used.
  - Wikidata occupant links: an end date (P582) or a dissolved organisation (P576) makes the entry 'former' and it is not counted; a start date and no end makes it 'current'; no qualifier makes it 'undated'.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz](data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz)
  - `data/raw/registry/wikidata-occupant-classes.json` (local, not committed)
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://qlever.dev/api/wikidata; SPARQL: P31 and P452 classes (English labels) of every Wikidata occupant item in registry/buildings.json; P466 and P159 statements of every registry building item with P580 and P582 qualifiers and the organisation's P576
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/categories.json](registry/categories.json)
  - `data/raw/registry/wikidata-occupant-classes.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: derive; after: `build-registry`, `registry-osm`
- Hand judgement: The rule tables (which OSM, FSA, CWG, register and Wikidata classes give which category) are written in the tool; mixed CWG labels are left out by judgement.
- Faults: [F8](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)
- Note: network is true only when the cache is missing or incomplete; tools/lib.mjs qlever paces the calls.

#### 79. `scan-model-pois` (tools/scan-model-pois.mjs)

- Command: `node --max-old-space-size=6000 magpie/cwplans/tools/scan-model-pois.mjs`
- Method: Lists the OSM points of interest of the October 2026 occupant categories (bar, alcohol, education, health, sport, arts) across the whole 3D model box and counts how many the building registry already has.
- Rules:
  - Model box -0.0950, 51.4740 to 0.0150, 51.5220 (fetch-docklands BOX_WGS84); registry box as registry-osm CW_BOX.
  - Classes: the OSM tag rules of build-categories for the six categories; never a name.
  - A way is placed at the mean of its nodes; a multipolygon at the mean of its first outer way known in the box.
  - in_registry: the OSM id is a registry occupant or building outline.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/model-box-pois.json](registry/model-box-pois.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: derive; after: `fetch-docklands-osm`, `build-registry`

### 8. Heritage and museum records

#### 12. `fetch_records` (registry/sources/museums/fetch_records.py)

- Command: `python3 magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/museums/fetch_records.py`
- Method: Collects geocoded museum objects, heritage records and archaeological catalogue records inside the Docklands box into one record list, and the APA polygons clipped to the box.
- Rules:
  - Wikidata objects of type human remains, skeleton, mummy or bog body, and abstract coin types, are left out.
  - Records outside the box are left out; duplicates (same source, id, relation, place, position) are dropped.
  - BNG points are converted with pyproj (Helmert, about 2 m; no OSTN15 grid).
  - ADS owner names are kept only for organisations in a fixed list; no finder, landowner, donor or author names are kept.
  - A record whose title, type, place or summary mentions burial, grave, cemetery, memorial and similar words gets the flag 'sensitive'.
  - Network: curl, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1, 0.6 s pause before every request, 300 s timeout; QLever queries wait 10 s, then 30, 50, 70 s before each of up to 4 tries.
  - Positions are rounded to 6 decimals; each record keeps the relation of its coordinate to the object (site, find spot, holding place, report about a site) and its precision.
  - Raw responses are saved to data/raw/registry/museums/ on every run; nothing is read back from them (no cache).
- Inputs:
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://qlever.dev/api/wikidata; SPARQL, three queries over the Docklands box: objects in a collection (P195) whose location (P276) is in the box; objects with their own P625 in the box; objects whose place of discovery (P189) or creation (P1071) is in the box; with label, P31, P571, P217, P195
  - **historic-england** (Historic England open data hub, OGL v3.0): https://services-eu1.arcgis.com/ZOdPfBS3aqqDYPUQ/arcgis/rest/services/; ArcGIS envelope queries: NHLE scheduled monuments (layer 6), scheduled wrecks (layer 24), research reports (layer 475), heritage harbours inventory (layer 0), Greater London Archaeological Priority Areas (layer 1, GeoJSON)
  - **ads-ariadne** (ADS / ARIADNE catalogue records, per record: OGL v3.0 or CC-BY (Portable Antiquities Scheme)): https://archaeologydataservice.ac.uk/data-catalogue-api/api/search; catalogue search in the box, 50 a page, six samples: publisher British Museum (PAS), contributor MOLA, subject Fieldwork report, subject Maritime, publisher and contributor Historic England
- Outputs:
  - `registry/sources/museums/records.json` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/sources/museums/apa_greater_london.geojson](registry/sources/museums/apa_greater_london.geojson)
  - `data/raw/registry/museums/wikidata_<query>.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/museums/he_<layer>.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/museums/he_apas.geojson` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/museums/ads_<sample>.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Note: Child processes: curl, ogr2ogr. The committed registry/sources/museums/records-open.json is a subset of records.json (CC0, OGL, CC-BY records without the sensitive flag, README "Published subset"), cut by hand (manual activity records-open-subset); no script in the repository writes it.

#### Hand step `records-open-subset`: Open-licence subset of the museum and heritage records

- By: hand; date: 2026-10-02
- Method: Cuts records-open.json from records.json (fetch_records.py): records under CC0 (Wikidata), OGL (Historic England, OGL-licensed ADS records) or CC BY (Portable Antiquities Scheme), without the "sensitive" flag (burial or memorial context).
- Rules:
  - Records flagged sensitive (44: burials, cemeteries, graves, memorials, skeletons) are left out (CLAUDE.md, Data ethics).
  - Records under other or unstated licences are left out.
- Inputs:
  - `registry/sources/museums/records.json` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/museums/records-open.json](registry/sources/museums/records-open.json)
- Decisions recorded in: registry/sources/museums/README.md, "Published subset"
- Not committed as code: The cut was made by hand; no script in the repository writes records-open.json.

### 9. Brands and branches

#### 19. `osm-scan` (registry/sources/brands/tools/osm-scan.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/brands/tools/osm-scan.mjs`
- Method: Lists every OSM feature in the Canary Wharf box that carries a business or name tag, placed at its node or at the mean of its nodes.
- Rules:
  - Kept: shop, amenity, office, tourism, leisure, craft, healthcare, brand, brand:wikidata or name.
  - Nodes within 0.003 degree of the box are read so ways at the edge get a position; a way or relation is kept when its mean position is in the box.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/registry/osm-box-features.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: clip; after: `fetch-docklands-osm`

#### 20. `london-brand-counts` (registry/sources/brands/tools/london-brand-counts.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/brands/tools/london-brand-counts.mjs`
- Method: Counts, for each brand:wikidata value, the OSM business features across Greater London (the brand size measure for the store-page check).
- Rules:
  - A feature counts when it has brand:wikidata and a shop, amenity, office, tourism, leisure, healthcare or craft key.
  - A ';' list of QIDs counts once for each QID.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/registry/london-brand-counts.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: derive; after: `fetch-docklands-osm`

#### 32. `build-uk-brands` (registry/sources/brands/tools/build-uk-brands.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/brands/tools/build-uk-brands.mjs`
- Method: Lists the NSI brand items whose location set, resolved by location-conflation, contains One Canada Square.
- Rules:
  - Only the brands/* tree of NSI.
  - Kept when the resolved locationSet contains [-0.0195, 51.5049].
  - scope 'uk' when the include list names GB, a GB subdivision or a custom .geojson area valid at the point; otherwise 'wider'.
- Inputs:
  - **nsi** (OSM name-suggestion-index 8.0.20260918, BSD-3-Clause): npm install name-suggestion-index@8.0.20260918 @rapideditor/location-conflation @rapideditor/country-coder (in data/raw/registry/nsi); the NSI package, installed by hand [manual download]
  - `data/raw/registry/nsi/node_modules/name-suggestion-index/dist/json/nsi.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/nsi/node_modules/name-suggestion-index/dist/json/featureCollection.json` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json](registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: nothing

#### 33. `wikidata-brands` (registry/sources/brands/tools/wikidata-brands.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/brands/tools/wikidata-brands.mjs`
- Method: Adds Wikidata facts (label, website, parent organisation, number of located items) to the NSI brand QIDs, and lists the items within 1 km of One Canada Square with any brand, owner, parent or operator link to an NSI brand.
- Rules:
  - nsi_brand_links: P1716, P127, P749 or P137 links whose target is an NSI UK brand QID.
  - Cache: each raw QLever answer is stored under the first 16 hex digits of the SHA-1 of the query in data/raw/registry/qlever/ and read back when present; delete a file to ask again.
  - Network: 0.7 s pause after each new query; no retry; User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1. Distances in metres, rounded to whole metres.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json](registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json)
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://qlever.dev/api/wikidata; SPARQL by VALUES (400 QIDs a query): English label, P856 websites, P749 parents with labels; count of items with P1716 = the brand and a P625
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://qlever.dev/api/wikidata; SPARQL spatialSearch (s2, 1000 m) around POINT(-0.0195 51.5049) over P625; then labels, P31 labels and P1716, P127, P749, P137 links of those items
  - `data/raw/registry/qlever/<sha1>.json` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/wikidata-brands.json](registry/sources/brands/wikidata-brands.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/wikidata-near.json](registry/sources/brands/wikidata-near.json)
  - `data/raw/registry/qlever/<sha1>.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `build-uk-brands`

#### 34. `match-osm` (registry/sources/brands/tools/match-osm.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/brands/tools/match-osm.mjs`
- Method: Reads the OSM business features of the Canary Wharf box (osm-scan) and keeps those that are branches of a brand: by the feature's brand:wikidata, by the NSI Matcher on its names under its own key=value at its position, or by a brand tag that NSI does not list; street furniture is counted apart.
- Rules:
  - A business feature is a branch when (1) its brand:wikidata is an NSI UK brand QID, or (2) the NSI Matcher matches its name, brand, name:en or official_name under its own key=value at its position, or (3) it has brand or brand:wikidata that NSI does not list.
  - For a QID with several NSI items, the item whose key=value agrees with the feature is preferred.
  - Disused features and opening_hours=closed are skipped.
  - Street furniture (post boxes, ATMs, bicycle rental, lockers, vending machines and similar) is counted apart, not as branches.
  - Name fields are tried in the order name, brand, name:en, official_name; the first NSI hit with match "primary" wins, else the first hit of any kind (alternate hits are accepted here, unlike the FSA match).
  - The NSI Matcher is asked at the feature's own position (lon, lat), not at a fixed point.
  - A conflict between the OSM brand:wikidata and the NSI QID is kept and flagged (qid_conflict); fhrs:id, addr:*, level, level:ref and website are carried for the later joins.
  - Named businesses that match no brand are listed apart (unmatched_named_business), not dropped silently.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/registry/osm-box-features.json` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json](registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json)
  - `data/raw/registry/nsi/node_modules/name-suggestion-index/dist/json/nsi.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/nsi/node_modules/name-suggestion-index/dist/json/featureCollection.json` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/registry/osm-branches.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: join; after: `osm-scan`, `build-uk-brands`

#### 35. `match-fsa-fetch` (registry/sources/brands/tools/match-fsa.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/brands/tools/match-fsa.mjs --fetch`
- Method: Matches the FSA premises found by postcodes-query in the Canary Wharf postcodes to NSI UK brands by name, fetches the address and position of each kept match, and writes the FSA branch list.
- Rules:
  - Premises: results[pc].fsa.items of postcodes/queries.json for postcodes in tiers cw-core, cw-ward, cw-box-other-ward.
  - Name variants (full, cut, strip, prefix) are tried in order; a hit must be 'primary' or 'alternate'.
  - Hits are ranked: primary before alternate, then key=value fitting the FSA business type, then scope uk.
  - A 'prefix' variant, an alternate hit or a type that does not fit needs a decision in fsa-decisions.json; undecided and rejected matches are listed apart.
  - Cache: each premises detail is stored in data/raw/registry/fsa-detail/<fhrs_id>.json and not fetched again.
  - Network: FSA API with x-api-version 2 and User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1, 0.6 s pause after each new detail; no retry.
- Inputs:
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
  - [postcodes/queries.json](postcodes/queries.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json](registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json](registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json)
  - `data/raw/registry/nsi/node_modules/name-suggestion-index/dist/json/nsi.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/nsi/node_modules/name-suggestion-index/dist/json/featureCollection.json` (local, not committed)
  - **fsa-api** (Food Standards Agency food hygiene rating API, OGL v3.0): https://api.ratings.food.gov.uk/Establishments/{fhrs_id}; address and geocode of each kept premises not yet cached
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/registry/fsa-branches.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/fsa-detail/<fhrs_id>.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: join; after: `postcodes-build`, `postcodes-query`, `build-uk-brands`
- Hand judgement: A prefix-variant, alternate or type-mismatch match is decided by hand in registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json (manual activity brand-decisions).

#### 36. `match-fsa` (registry/sources/brands/tools/match-fsa.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/brands/tools/match-fsa.mjs`
- Method: The same match as match-fsa-fetch without network: addresses and positions come only from the cached details.
- Rules:
  - As match-fsa-fetch.
- Inputs:
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
  - [postcodes/queries.json](postcodes/queries.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json](registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json](registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json)
  - `data/raw/registry/nsi/node_modules/name-suggestion-index/dist/json/nsi.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/nsi/node_modules/name-suggestion-index/dist/json/featureCollection.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/fsa-detail/<fhrs_id>.json` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/registry/fsa-branches.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: join; after: `postcodes-build`, `postcodes-query`, `build-uk-brands`, `match-fsa-fetch`
- Hand judgement: Same decisions as match-fsa-fetch (registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json).

#### 70. `build-branches` (registry/sources/brands/tools/build-branches.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/brands/tools/build-branches.mjs`
- Method: Merges the OSM, FSA, Wikidata, CWG directory and store-page evidence into one list of chain-store branches in the Canary Wharf box, with sources, position, mall, level and a confidence.
- Rules:
  - Records join only when they are the same brand family (same Wikidata QID or a P749 parent, else same NSI id) and the same place: within 120 m (160 m when a position is approximate), or the same postcode, or the same mall.
  - Two named malls decide a join by themselves; Canada Place and One Canada Square count as one level.
  - An OSM feature whose fhrs:id equals the FSA premises id is the same branch.
  - Wikidata items join a branch within 300 m.
  - A CWG entry needs an NSI match (or a decision in cwg-decisions.json); a 'prefix' or 'alternate' match or a type that does not fit the CWG section is left undecided; otherwise a CWG title that names a branch already found (5 or more letters, whole words) joins it.
  - Postcode to mall: kept when 4 or more CWG entries share the postcode and 85% or more name the same mall.
  - Position: OSM feature, else FSA geocode, else mall centre or ONSPD postcode centroid (flagged).
  - Confidence: high with two or more sources; low for a single prefix or alternate FSA match or a note that says low confidence; else medium.
  - Category from the key=value (bank, pharmacy, supermarket, cafe, takeaway, restaurant, bar, gym, hotel, leisure, health, childcare, office, motoring, service, shop, other).
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/registry/osm-branches.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/fsa-branches.json` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/wikidata-near.json](registry/sources/brands/wikidata-near.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/wikidata-brands.json](registry/sources/brands/wikidata-brands.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json](registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-decisions.json](registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-decisions.json)
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json](registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json)
  - `data/raw/registry/nsi/node_modules/name-suggestion-index/dist/json/nsi.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/nsi/node_modules/name-suggestion-index/dist/json/featureCollection.json` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: join; after: `match-osm`, `match-fsa-fetch`, `fetch-cwg`, `wikidata-brands`, `build-uk-brands`, `postcodes-build`, `storelocator`
- Hand judgement: CWG matches that the rules leave undecided are decided by hand in registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-decisions.json (manual activity brand-decisions).
- Faults: [F2](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F3](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F7](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F9](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)
- Note: Runs twice (brands README): a first merge, then storelocator, then a final merge that attaches the store pages. The 'after' cycle with storelocator is real.

#### 71. `storelocator` (registry/sources/brands/tools/storelocator.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/brands/tools/storelocator.mjs`
- Method: Checks the brand's own page for each Canary Wharf branch of the 30 largest brand families present in the box.
- Rules:
  - Brand family: a brand and its Wikidata parent count as one; size = the largest Greater London OSM branch count of the family.
  - Candidate URL: the manual entry for the branch, else OSM website=* when it is not canarywharf.com and not a bare home page.
  - verified: HTTP 2xx, no bot-challenge page, and the page text or the URL path names Canary Wharf, the postcode or the mall; 401, 403, 429 or a challenge page = blocked.
  - A URL with a stored status is not fetched again.
  - Cache: data/raw/registry/storelocator-cache.json holds each URL's result and is written after every fetch.
  - Network: 1.1 s minimum gap per host, 60 s timeout, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; blocked pages are recorded, not retried.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
  - `data/raw/registry/london-brand-counts.json` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/sources/brands/wikidata-brands.json](registry/sources/brands/wikidata-brands.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/tools/storelocator-manual.json](registry/sources/brands/tools/storelocator-manual.json)
  - `data/raw/registry/storelocator-cache.json` (local, not committed)
  - **brand-sites** (brand store-locator pages, none stated): the branch's store page URL (OSM website=* below the brand's domain, or a URL found by hand); one GET per URL, identifying User-Agent, redirects followed, at most 1 request a second per host
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json](registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json)
  - `data/raw/registry/storelocator-cache.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `build-branches`, `london-brand-counts`, `wikidata-brands`
- Hand judgement: Store-page URLs that OSM does not give were found by hand on the brand locators: registry/sources/brands/tools/storelocator-manual.json (manual activity storelocator-manual).

#### 72. `readme-tables` (registry/sources/brands/tools/readme-tables.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/brands/tools/readme-tables.mjs > /tmp/tables.md`
- Method: Prints the branch table, the top brands and the unmatched CWG entries as Markdown for the brands README.
- Rules:
  - One row per branch: brand, category, name, where (mall, level, postcode or address), sources, confidence, links.
  - The output is pasted into registry/sources/brands/README.md by hand; the script writes to standard output only.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/README.md](registry/sources/brands/README.md)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: export; after: `build-branches`

#### Hand step `brand-decisions`: Hand decisions on FSA and CWG brand matches

- By: hand; date: 2026-10-02/03
- Method: Matches that the rules cannot decide (an NSI prefix variant, an alternate name, a business type that does not fit the brand) are judged by hand, one record at a time, and written to decision files that the tools read on every run.
- Rules:
  - A decision names the record, the brand item and accept or reject, with a reason.
  - Undecided and rejected matches are listed apart by the tools, never joined.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json](registry/sources/brands/brands-uk.json)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json](registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-decisions.json](registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-decisions.json)
- Decisions recorded in: registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json; registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-decisions.json

#### Hand step `storelocator-manual`: Store-page URLs found by hand

- By: hand; date: 2026-10-02/03
- Method: For branches whose OSM website is missing or a bare home page, the branch page was found by hand on the brand's own locator and its URL recorded; storelocator.mjs then checks it like any other.
- Rules:
  - A URL on the brand's own domain; the UK site first.
  - Only the URL is recorded, not page content.
- Inputs:
  - **brand-sites** (brand store-locator pages, none stated)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/tools/storelocator-manual.json](registry/sources/brands/tools/storelocator-manual.json)
- Decisions recorded in: registry/sources/brands/tools/storelocator-manual.json

### 10. Canary Wharf Group directory

#### 13. `fetch-cwg` (registry/sources/brands/tools/fetch-cwg.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/brands/tools/fetch-cwg.mjs`
- Method: Reads the Canary Wharf Group directory from Internet Archive copies (the live site blocks scripts) and extracts each entry's title, categories, address lines, mall, level, postcode website and street line, with flags for entries that are not one place.
- Rules:
  - Capture order per entry: its own URL from the CDX list, its 2025 URL (eating-drinking/ or shops-services/ directory), then the capture closest to 2026-03-04.
  - Captures of the Incapsula bot-challenge page are skipped and counted.
  - Fields from the address lines (registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-fields.mjs): mall = the first name of a fixed place list found in the lines, but never a name inside a longer one that is also found ("Upper Bank Street", not "Bank Street"); level = the first line that names a level, floor, mezzanine, podium or kiosk, or starts with "Lower Mall", "Upper Mall" or "Unit" (not "Upper Bank Street", not "United Kingdom"); postcode = the first full UK postcode in any district (Wood Wharf uses E22); street = the first line that starts with a house number or One/Two/Three; flags: template field, estate-wide, car park. Fixed 2026-10-03: the first parser read 4 levels and 1 mall wrongly and missed 5 malls and 3 E22 postcodes.
  - Raw pages are cached and not fetched again.
  - Categories: every tag div between the category block and the title (the first parser found none on any page; 339 entries have one now).
  - Network: User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1, 120 s timeout, 1.2 s pause after every fetched page, up to 5 tries (CDX listings and copies with no CDX timestamp: 2) with 8 s x try between tries; HTTP 404 is no copy.
  - Owner rule 2026-10-03: crawls through the Internet Archive are fair use for scoping (CLAUDE.md, cwplans exception).
- Inputs:
  - **cwg-site** (Canary Wharf Group directory pages, none stated (all rights reserved by default)): https://web.archive.org/web/20260304id_/https://canarywharf.com/{shop,restaurant,stay,seedo}-sitemap.xml; the site's Yoast sitemaps as archived on 2026-03-04 (the directory entries)
  - **cwg-site** (Canary Wharf Group directory pages, none stated (all rights reserved by default)): https://web.archive.org/cdx/search/cdx?url=canarywharf.com/<prefix>/&matchType=prefix&from=2025; CDX capture listings for six URL prefixes (status 200, no query strings)
  - **cwg-site** (Canary Wharf Group directory pages, none stated (all rights reserved by default)): https://web.archive.org/web/<timestamp>id_/<entry url>; the archived copy of each directory entry page
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - `data/raw/registry/cwg/` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Faults: [F14](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

### 11. Registers

#### 37. `fetch-registers` (tools/fetch-registers.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-registers.mjs [gias|cqc|ods|charities|ofsted-childcare|gambling|active-places|fsa-pubs ...] [--refresh]`
- Method: Downloads each register (bulk file or query), keeps the records with an E14 postcode or a source coordinate in the registry box, and writes one {meta, records} file per source with the source URL, fetch date, licence, method, counts and the fields dropped.
- Rules:
  - Kept: postcode district E14, or a source coordinate inside CW_BOX [-0.03, 51.498, -0.005, 51.51].
  - Position: the source coordinate when there is one (Active Places lat/long; GIAS Easting/Northing through OSTN15; FSA geocode); else the ONSPD centre of the postcode, marked position = 'postcode centre (ONSPD)'. in_box follows the same position.
  - Dropped everywhere: telephone numbers, email addresses, named contact people (Active Places site contacts, GIAS head teachers, CQC registered managers are not in the file used), and Ofsted 'registered person' names unless they look like an organisation.
  - Ofsted rows REDACTED by Ofsted (childminders and home childcarers) are not kept.
  - GIAS rows administered from an E14 correspondence address (Fieldwork Overseas Establishments; c/o street lines) get address_role; they are not schools in E14.
  - Gambling register rows have no premises id: id = gc:<operator account>:<postcode>:<n>.
  - Network: one request at a time per host, at least 1 s apart; retries on 429 and 5xx with a doubling pause (Retry-After honoured); User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1.
- Inputs:
  - **dfe-gias** (DfE Get Information About Schools, OGL v3.0): https://ea-edubase-api-prod.azurewebsites.net/edubase/downloads/public/edubasealldata<YYYYMMDD>.csv; the newest daily all-establishments CSV (today, else up to 9 days back)
  - **cqc-directory** (Care Quality Commission care directory, OGL v3.0): https://www.cqc.org.uk/about-us/transparency/using-cqc-data; the page, to find the current *_CQC_directory.csv link; then that CSV (about 19 MB)
  - **nhs-ods** (NHS Organisation Data Service, ORD API 2-0-0, OGL v3.0): https://directory.spineservices.nhs.uk/ORD/2-0-0/organisations; ?PostCode=E14&Limit=1000 (one list), then /organisations/<OrgId> for each (422 on 2026-10-03), and /ORD/2-0-0/roles
  - **charity-commission** (Charity Commission for England and Wales, register of charities full extract, OGL v3.0): https://ccewuksprdoneregsadata1.blob.core.windows.net/data/txt/publicextract.charity.zip; the daily full extract (about 44 MB, tab-separated)
  - **ofsted-childcare-mi** (Ofsted management information: childcare providers and inspections, provider-level CSV, OGL v3.0): https://www.gov.uk/api/content/government/statistical-data-sets/childcare-providers-and-inspections-management-information; the attachment list; then the newest 'childcare provider(s) data as at' CSV (about 19 MB)
  - **gambling-commission-premises** (Gambling Commission register of gambling premises, OGL (stated on the Commission's data.gov.uk record 8dc9bef0-ad46-497b-9a74-30a6ce9d2f98; the download page states none); re-check with the Commission before production): https://www.gamblingcommission.gov.uk/downloads/premises-licence-register.csv; the whole register CSV (about 1.3 MB)
  - **sport-england-active-places** (Sport England Active Places, CC BY 4.0 (personal data excluded from the licence)): https://services-eu1.arcgis.com/s9MgJChYyPlPX2Nk/arcgis/rest/services/GIS_Active_Places_Power_Sites/FeatureServer/0/query; where=postcode LIKE 'E14%'; and an envelope query on the registry box; then GIS_Active_Places_Power_Facility/FeatureServer/1/query where siteid IN (batches of 100)
  - [data/raw/registry/fhrs/FHRS530-2026-10-03.json.gz](data/raw/registry/fhrs/FHRS530-2026-10-03.json.gz)
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/registers/gias.json](registry/sources/registers/gias.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/cqc.json](registry/sources/registers/cqc.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/ods.json](registry/sources/registers/ods.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/charities.json](registry/sources/registers/charities.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/ofsted-childcare.json](registry/sources/registers/ofsted-childcare.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/gambling.json](registry/sources/registers/gambling.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/active-places.json](registry/sources/registers/active-places.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/fsa-pubs.json](registry/sources/registers/fsa-pubs.json)
  - `data/raw/registers/` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `postcodes-build`, `registry-fhrs`, `fetch-raw-grid`
- Hand judgement: Which registers to take and their licence reading are recorded in registry/sources/registers/README.md (licence notes, rejected sources).
- Faults: [F7](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F17](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F18](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)
- Note: Child process: unzip -p (charities). Not reached: the Tower Hamlets premises licence register (alcohol-entertainment.towerhamlets.gov.uk: TLS handshake failure and HTTP 503 on 2026-10-03); see registry/sources/registers/README.md.

### 12. Web crawl

#### 74. `crawl-sites` (tools/crawl-sites.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/crawl-sites.mjs`
- Method: Fetches the web pages the registry and the brand sources already link to and extracts structured facts: schema.org JSON-LD and microdata (businesses, organisations, events), OpenGraph, page language, HTTP status and final URL, feeds, booking/menu/events links, and whether the page names the entry's Canary Wharf postcode or address.
- Rules:
  - Skipped: FSA ratings pages (data from the FSA API), OSM, Wikidata, Companies House and Land Registry links (data from their APIs or bulk files), Wikipedia (CC BY-SA, share-alike), canarywharf.com pages with no archived copy (the live site answers scripts with an Imperva challenge).
  - A canarywharf.com URL is replaced by its Internet Archive copy from cwg-directory.json.
  - robots.txt per RFC 9309: the group for 'glitchcan-cwplans', else '*'; longest match wins, Allow wins a tie; a 4xx robots.txt allows all; a 5xx or unreachable robots.txt disallows all.
  - Failure classes: robots disallowed, robots unreachable, bot challenge (Cloudflare, Imperva, Akamai, DataDome, PerimeterX pages), 401/403 refused, 404/410 not found, 4xx other, 429, 5xx, timeout, dns, tls, connection, not HTML, too many redirects.
  - Kept per page: schema.org name, telephone, address, geo, opening hours, price range, cuisine, menu, sameAs, reservations; events with name, dates, location, URL; page title and OpenGraph title/type (cut to 200 characters), OpenGraph description cut to 200 characters; html lang; feed and link URLs. No other page text.
  - names_branch: true when the entry's postcode is in the page text or structured data, or an address phrase (street, mall, branch name) is on a page that is not the site's home page; 'home page lists the place' when only a phrase is on a home page; 'scripts only' when the match is only inside page scripts.
  - A URL with a stored result is not fetched again (--refresh fetches all; --retry-failed fetches transient failures again).
  - At most 8 redirects (MAX_REDIRECTS); the backoff waits Retry-After when given, else 4, 12, 36 s.
  - Owner rule 2026-10-03: website crawls, direct and through the Internet Archive or Common Crawl, are fair use for scoping (CLAUDE.md, cwplans exception).
- Inputs:
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json](registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - [feeds/events.json](feeds/events.json)
  - [feeds/feeds.json](feeds/feeds.json)
  - `data/raw/registry/cwg/pages/` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/crawl/` (local, not committed)
  - **brand-sites** (brand store-locator pages, none stated): occupants[].website and .store_url in buildings.json; branches[].website, .cwg_website, .store_url; storelocator checked[].store_url; cwg-directory directory[].website; robots.txt once per host, then one GET per URL with redirects followed by hand (robots.txt checked on each new host), User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1, one request at a time per host at least 1.5 s apart, at most 4 hosts at once, 20 s timeout, up to 4 tries with backoff on 429, 5xx, timeouts and resets
  - **cwg-site** (Canary Wharf Group directory pages, none stated (all rights reserved by default)): https://web.archive.org/web/<timestamp>id_/https://canarywharf.com/<kind>/<slug>/; the archived copy named in cwg-directory.json; the copy fetch-cwg.mjs saved in data/raw/registry/cwg/pages/ is reused, so no request is made when it exists
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/web/site-facts.json](registry/sources/web/site-facts.json)
  - [registry/sources/web/discovered-feeds.json](registry/sources/web/discovered-feeds.json)
  - `data/raw/crawl/<host>/<hash>.html.gz, <hash>.json, robots.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `fetch-cwg`, `storelocator`, `build-branches`, `build-registry`
- Hand judgement: The skip list (FSA pages, OSM, Wikidata, Companies House, Land Registry, Wikipedia) is a licence and source-of-truth decision written in the tool.

### 13. Structured data (Factoidal)

#### 75. `render-structured-data` (tools/render-structured-data.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/render-structured-data.mjs [--retry-failed] [--storefinder-only] [--build-only]`
- Method: Renders every web page the registry links to in headless Chromium and extracts from the rendered DOM: every JSON-LD script text as found, microdata (WHATWG algorithm) as JSON, and RDFa 1.1 as N-Triples. The same extractor runs over the server HTML of the same visit with scripts off, to mark blocks that only scripts made. For chain branches with no store page, searches the brand's store finder with the branch postcode and renders the branch page it links to.
- Rules:
  - Skipped: FSA ratings pages, OSM, Wikidata, Wikipedia (CC BY-SA), Companies House, Land Registry, register pages, social networks, the live canarywharf.com (Imperva challenge).
  - robots.txt per RFC 9309 (token glitchcan-cwplans, else *); an unreachable robots.txt means no visit; the failure is classed by its cause (dns, tls, timeout, 5xx).
  - Order: store pages, then pages the plain crawl got no structured data from, then the rest; Internet Archive CWG copies read from cache with scripts off.
  - A cookie banner is clicked only when the page shows no structured data and a known consent button is visible. No logins, no forms other than a store-finder search box with a UK postcode.
  - Store finder: a result link is followed only when its text, URL or card names the branch postcode, a street or mall phrase of the branch, or (for a brand with one branch here) Canary Wharf.
  - Blocks over 200 kB are dropped and counted.
  - User-Agent: a desktop Chrome string plus " glitchcan-cwplans/0.1 (+https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam)"; robots.txt fetched with Node (15 s timeout), else through Chromium.
  - Each page: wait for load and network idle, at most 20 s (CAP_MS), then 1.5 s (LATE_MS) for late JSON-LD.
  - Store finders: one postcode search per brand and postcode; the postcode is the branch's own, else E14 4QT (owner rule 2026-10-03: "we are permitted per industry convention to submit storefinder forms with UK postcodes"). No other forms.
  - Resumable: a URL with a stored result in data/raw/rendered/ is not rendered again unless --retry-failed and its failure was transient.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json](registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/wikidata-brands.json](registry/sources/brands/wikidata-brands.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/charities.json](registry/sources/registers/charities.json)
  - [registry/sources/web/site-facts.json](registry/sources/web/site-facts.json)
  - [tools/structured-dom.mjs](tools/structured-dom.mjs)
  - `data/raw/crawl/<host>/robots.json and <hash>.html.gz (robots rules and server HTML of the plain crawl, when present)` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/registry/cwg/pages/` (local, not committed)
  - **web-structured-data** (schema.org structured data, none stated (all rights reserved by default); the site owners publish this markup for search engines): occupants[].website, .contact:website, .cwg_website, .store_url (buildings.json); branches[].website, .cwg_website, .store_url; storelocator checked[].store_url; cwg-directory directory[].website; charities records[].website (registered charities); brand sites from Wikidata P856 for store finders; robots.txt once per host (Node fetch, else Chromium), then one headless Chromium page load per URL; robots.txt checked for every main-frame navigation; at most 3 pages at once, one per host, at least 2 s apart; images, media and fonts blocked; store finders: home page, up to three finder pages, one postcode search per brand and postcode, the branch page
- Outputs:
  - [../../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages](../../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages)
  - [../../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/index.json](../../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/index.json)
  - `data/raw/rendered/<host>/<sha1(url)[0:16]>.html.gz (rendered DOM), .json (result), robots.json; data/raw/rendered/_storefinder/` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `crawl-sites`
- Hand judgement: The skip list and the consent-button list are written in the tool; store-finder searches follow the owner rule of 2026-10-03.

#### 76. `extract-structured-data` (tools/extract-structured-data.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/extract-structured-data.mjs`
- Method: Cleans each JSON-LD block (jsonld-clean.mjs: wrappers, comments, trailing and missing commas, control characters, invalid escapes, concatenated values, HTML entities, remote schema.org contexts inlined as {"@vocab": "https://schema.org/"}, @graph unwrapped, http://schema.org/ normalised; every repair counted by class), converts it with Factoidal jsonldToRdf, converts microdata JSON to RDF (schema.org vocabulary), parses RDFa N-Triples with Factoidal, loads one named graph per page, writes all.nq, then runs SPARQL with Factoidal for types, names, telephones, opening hours and their specifications, addresses, geo and events, and attributes each node to the registry entities that link to the page.
- Rules:
  - Scope branch, confidence high: the node's postcode equals the entity's, or its geo is within 300 m of the building.
  - Scope branch, confidence medium: the node has no address and the page is the branch's own page (a store_url field, a store-finder result, or a URL path that names the place).
  - Scope branch, confidence low: the node's postcode is another E14/E20 postcode (a sibling branch is possible).
  - Scope chain: a chain branch's general page with no address on the node (chain-wide facts, not branch facts). Scope organisation: the same for a non-chain entity's own site.
  - Nodes with an address or position elsewhere are not attributed and are counted.
  - Opening hours to OSM opening_hours: days grouped, 23:59 closes become 24:00, opens = closes = 00:00 is 'off', dated specifications become date-range rules; unparsed text is kept raw with the problem listed.
- Inputs:
  - [../../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages](../../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/pages)
  - [../../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/index.json](../../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/index.json)
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json](registry/sources/brands/storelocator.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/charities.json](registry/sources/registers/charities.json)
  - [tools/jsonld-clean.mjs](tools/jsonld-clean.mjs)
  - **factoidal** (@factoidal/core 0.7.1, Apache-2.0): npm @factoidal/core 0.7.1 (local); jsonldToRdf, parse (N-Triples, N-Quads), query (SPARQL 1.1 SELECT over named graphs)
- Outputs:
  - [../../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz](../../third_party/cwplans-structured-data/all.nq.gz)
  - [registry/sources/web/structured-facts.json](registry/sources/web/structured-facts.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `render-structured-data`

#### 77. `join-web-facts` (tools/join-web-facts.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/join-web-facts.mjs`
- Method: Adds the facts read from the entities' own web pages (schema.org opening hours, telephone, events, menu, price range, cuisine) to the building occupants as occupants[].web, with the page, the fetch date, the scope and the confidence of the match.
- Rules:
  - Keys: "cwb-NNNN|name" (occupant name in that building, case and accents folded), "branch:<brand QID>@<element>" (the occupant's OSM element, the building's OSM way, fhrs/<FSA id> or a bare FSA id, cwg/<slug>), "charity:<number>", "cwg:<directory slug>".
  - One record per occupant: branch scope beats organisation beats chain; then high, medium, low confidence.
  - A fact record with no hours, phone, events, menu, price range or cuisine is not joined; records that match no occupant are counted (summary.joins.web_facts).
  - Run after build-registry (which rewrites buildings.json without web facts) and before build-categories and build-atlas.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/sources/web/structured-facts.json](registry/sources/web/structured-facts.json)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `build-registry`, `extract-structured-data`

### 14. Atlas index

#### 80. `build-atlas` (tools/build-atlas.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/build-atlas.mjs`
- Method: Builds the compact index the atlas page loads first: buildings with outlines and model heights, postcodes with company and home totals, heritage groups, riverbed soundings, wrecks, flood defences, Wikidata items, published levels and overview counts.
- Rules:
  - Model buildings (area.js) belong to a registry building when their vertex mean lies inside its outline; the tallest gives the model height and source.
  - Heritage records in the Docklands box are grouped by position rounded to 5 decimals.
  - Soundings: z_cd minus the chart datum offset interpolated by longitude between 3.20 m and 3.35 m (same rule as build-docklands).
  - Defence crest: actual_ucl, else actual_dcl, else design_ucl; a line is kept when one of its points is in the box.
  - Wikidata items without an English label are left out.
  - Levels: the sourced levels used by the 3D build, CWG structures (slabs, water, dock bed), the deepest and median sounding off the Isle of Dogs, and the 8 tallest named buildings (Wikidata, else OSM, else LiDAR height on the LiDAR ground, or 5 m OD when unknown, marked approx).
  - Rounding: outlines as microdegree deltas (1e-6 degree, about 0.1 m); positions to 6 decimals; heights and levels to 0.01 m; heritage groups by position rounded to 5 decimals.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/categories.json](registry/categories.json)
  - [data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz](data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz)
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
  - [registry/companies-by-postcode.json](registry/companies-by-postcode.json)
  - [registry/homes-by-postcode.json](registry/homes-by-postcode.json)
  - [registry/sources/museums/records-open.json](registry/sources/museums/records-open.json)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_teddington_greenwich_25m.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_teddington_greenwich_25m.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_greenwich_coalhouse_50m.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_greenwich_coalhouse_50m.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_hms_belfast_berth.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_thames_hms_belfast_berth.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/pla/ukho_wrecks_obstructions_points.geojson](registry/sources/pla/ukho_wrecks_obstructions_points.geojson)
  - [data/raw/docklands/ea-defences.json.gz](data/raw/docklands/ea-defences.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/docklands/wikidata-items.json.gz](data/raw/docklands/wikidata-items.json.gz)
  - [data/sourced-levels.json](data/sourced-levels.json)
  - [docklands/facts.json](docklands/facts.json)
  - [feeds/feeds.json](feeds/feeds.json)
  - [feeds/events.json](feeds/events.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
  - [data-register.json](data-register.json)
- Outputs:
  - [atlas/data/atlas.json](atlas/data/atlas.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: join; after: `build-registry`, `build-categories`, `build-docklands`, `postcodes-build`, `fetch_records`, `fetch_wet-soundings`, `fetch-docklands-defences`, `fetch-docklands-wikidata`, `build-branches`, `registry-osm`, `fetch-raw-grid`
- Faults: [F5](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

### 15. Trees and greens

#### 59. `fetch-trees` (tools/build-trees.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/build-trees.mjs fetch [gla|osgs|tpo|tow ...]`
- Method: Downloads the open tree inventories and green-space layers for the Docklands box into data/raw/trees/ (gitignored).
- Rules:
  - One request at a time, at least 2 s apart; retried with a doubling pause on 429 or 5xx.
  - The GLA CSV and the OS ZIP are written as fetched; the planning.data and TOW responses are concatenated pages (features with their properties and geometry).
- Inputs:
  - **gla-public-realm-trees** (GLA London Public Realm Trees, November 2025 release, OGL v3.0): https://data.london.gov.uk/download/2r45m/e62a6a1f-390d-4193-ae32-3aabd9846f36/Borough_tree_list_2025Nov.csv; the whole London Public Realm Trees CSV, November 2025 release (208 MB)
  - **os-open-greenspace** (OS Open Greenspace, version 2026-04, OGL v3.0 (OS OpenData licence: http://os.uk/opendata/licence redirects to OGL v3)): https://api.os.uk/downloads/v1/products/OpenGreenspace/downloads?area=TQ&format=ESRI%C2%AE+Shapefile&redirect; the TQ tile of OS Open Greenspace as ESRI Shapefile (5 MB ZIP)
  - **planning-data-tree** (planning.data.gov.uk dataset 'tree', OGL v3.0): https://www.planning.data.gov.uk/entity.json; dataset=tree, geometry = the model box (WGS84 polygon), geometry_relation=intersects, 500 a page until a short page
  - **fr-tow** (Forest Research National Trees Outside Woodland map V1, London, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/spatialdata/national-trees-outside-woodland-map/ogc/features/v1/collections/FR_TOW_V1_London/items; f=json, bbox = the model box in WGS84, limit=10000, startIndex 0, 10000, 20000
- Outputs:
  - `data/raw/trees/gla-public-realm-trees-2025Nov.csv` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/opgrsp_essh_tq.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/planning-data-tree.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/tow-london-box.json.gz` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing

#### 60. `build-trees` (tools/build-trees.mjs)

- Command: `node --max-old-space-size=6000 magpie/cwplans/tools/build-trees.mjs`
- Method: Joins the tree inventories, OSM trees and the Trees Outside Woodland lone-tree crowns into one list of trees in model metres, with OS Open Greenspace sites, OSM green areas and TOW canopy groups as green rings.
- Rules:
  - Box: BNG E 532400-539900, N 176700-182300 (fetch-docklands.mjs BOX_BNG); WGS84 to BNG through OSTN15; x = E - 537550, z = -(N - 180300); every value rounded to 0.5 m.
  - Ethics (CLAUDE.md, Data ethics): a tree keeps only position, taxon (scientific and common name), height, crown spread, source and record id. Notes, sponsorship, memorial, dedication, inscription, address, owner and maintainer fields are never read into the payload; each source lists its dropped fields in sources[].dropped.
  - Tree precedence: GLA public realm inventory first; then TPO points (planning.data), OSM natural=tree nodes and TOW Lone Tree crowns, each added only if no tree already kept lies within 3 m (TOW: within the crown radius, at least 3 m).
  - GLA class values ("05 to 10 m") become the middle of the class; "Not recorded" is null. Species "Unknown Tree Species" and common name "Plant" give no species.
  - TPO: records with an end date are left out; the taxon is read only from Tower Hamlets names of the form "Common (Scientific)"; Southwark names (site descriptions, URLs) are dropped.
  - OSM: species, else taxon, else genus (with the :en names); height under 60 m and diameter_crown under 40 m kept.
  - TOW Lone Tree: centroid of the polygon, height = maxht, crown = diameter of a circle with the polygon area (tow_area_m). Group of Trees, Small Woodland and NFI OHC polygons become greens with meanht.
  - Greens are clipped to the box (Sutherland-Hodgman) and simplified (Douglas-Peucker 0.5 m; TOW 1.5 m, its outlines follow the 1 m LiDAR grid); OSM multipolygon inner rings and shapefile holes are kept as holes.
  - Network for the source files: see fetch-trees.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/trees/gla-public-realm-trees-2025Nov.csv` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/opgrsp_essh_tq.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/planning-data-tree.json` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/trees/tow-london-box.json.gz` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/trees.json](docklands/data/trees.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `fetch-trees`, `fetch-docklands-osm`, `fetch-raw-grid`
- Hand judgement: The 35 m cut-off for "lone trees" that are cranes or structures is applied on the page, not in the file; the source precedence order is a judgement.

### 16. Imagery and page assets

#### 39. `build-imagery` (tools/build-imagery.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/build-imagery.mjs [from-date]`
- Method: Colours each terrain vertex of the Docklands model from the least cloudy recent Sentinel-2 true-colour image.
- Rules:
  - Model metres to BNG to UTM with a Helmert transform (about 2 m, inside a 10 m pixel).
  - Each vertex takes the mean of the pixels within half a terrain cell of it.
  - Network: one STAC search, then HTTP range requests for the pixel window only; User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; no retry; no cache. The scene id and date are written into imagery.js.
  - Colours are 8-bit means, rounded to whole values.
- Inputs:
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
  - **copernicus-sentinel** (Copernicus Sentinel-2 L2A, Copernicus Sentinel data: free, full and open (attribution)): https://earth-search.aws.element84.com/v1/search; STAC search: collection sentinel-2-l2a intersecting POINT(-0.0195 51.5049) from the from-date (default 120 days before the run) to now, eo:cloud_cover < 10, sorted by cloud cover, limit 1
  - **copernicus-sentinel** (Copernicus Sentinel-2 L2A, Copernicus Sentinel data: free, full and open (attribution)): the scene's 'visual' (TCI) Cloud-Optimised GeoTIFF on S3; HTTP range requests for the pixel window over the model box only
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/imagery.js](docklands/data/imagery.js)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: derive; after: `build-docklands`

#### 40. `build-aerial-rgb2008` (tools/build-aerial.py)

- Command: `python3 magpie/cwplans/tools/build-aerial.py rgb2008`
- Method: Decodes the 2008 colour aerial photographs, pastes them onto one canvas over the model box at 3 m a pixel and saves a JPEG.
- Rules:
  - Model box x -5150 to 2350, z -2000 to 3600 (local metres), 3 m a pixel.
  - ECW decoded at every 5th pixel; black (0,0,0) outside the flown area is not pasted; one pixel of overlap between tiles.
- Inputs:
  - **ea-dsm-history** (Environment Agency LiDAR DSM survey tiles 1999 to 2022, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/tiles/collections/survey/vertical_aerial_photography_tiles_rgb/2008/0.4/<tile>; tiles TQ3075, TQ3080, TQ3575, TQ3580 (ECW in ZIP) [manual download]
  - `data/raw/imagery/vertical_aerial_photography_tiles_rgb-2008-0.4-*.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `tools/native/ecw2ppm` (local, not committed)
  - [docklands/data/tex/textures.json](docklands/data/tex/textures.json)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/tex/rgb2008.jpg](docklands/data/tex/rgb2008.jpg)
  - [docklands/data/tex/textures.json](docklands/data/tex/textures.json)
  - `data/raw/imagery/rgb2008.ppm` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: nothing

#### 41. `build-aerial-night2012` (tools/build-aerial.py)

- Command: `python3 magpie/cwplans/tools/build-aerial.py night2012`
- Method: As build-aerial-rgb2008 for the 2012 night-time aerial photographs.
- Rules:
  - ECW decoded at every 10th pixel; black outside the flown area is not pasted.
- Inputs:
  - **ea-dsm-history** (Environment Agency LiDAR DSM survey tiles 1999 to 2022, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/tiles/collections/survey/vertical_aerial_photography_tiles_night_time/2012/0.2/<tile>; tiles TQ3075, TQ3080, TQ3575, TQ3580 (ECW in ZIP) [manual download]
  - `data/raw/imagery/vertical_aerial_photography_tiles_night_time-2012-0.2-*.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `tools/native/ecw2ppm` (local, not committed)
  - [docklands/data/tex/textures.json](docklands/data/tex/textures.json)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/tex/night2012.jpg](docklands/data/tex/night2012.jpg)
  - [docklands/data/tex/textures.json](docklands/data/tex/textures.json)
  - `data/raw/imagery/night2012.ppm` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: nothing

#### 42. `build-aerial-intensity2020` (tools/build-aerial.py)

- Command: `python3 magpie/cwplans/tools/build-aerial.py intensity2020`
- Method: Mosaics the 2020 LiDAR intensity tiles over the model box at 3 m a pixel, stretches them and saves a grey JPEG.
- Rules:
  - Negative values are set to 0.
  - Stretch between the 1st and 99th percentile of the non-zero pixels (on a 0-4096 scale), then gamma 0.6.
- Inputs:
  - **ea-dsm-history** (Environment Agency LiDAR DSM survey tiles 1999 to 2022, OGL v3.0): https://environment.data.gov.uk/tiles/collections/survey/national_lidar_programme_intensity/2020/1/<tile>; tiles TQ3075, TQ3080, TQ3575, TQ3580 (GeoTIFF with TFW in ZIP) [manual download]
  - `data/raw/imagery/national_lidar_programme_intensity-2020-1-*.zip` (local, not committed)
  - [docklands/data/tex/textures.json](docklands/data/tex/textures.json)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/tex/intensity2020.jpg](docklands/data/tex/intensity2020.jpg)
  - [docklands/data/tex/textures.json](docklands/data/tex/textures.json)
  - `data/raw/imagery/intensity2020.ppm` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: nothing

#### 57. `check-fp16-shaders` (tools/check-fp16-shaders.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/check-fp16-shaders.mjs [--no-browser]`
- Method: Checks the 3D page's shaders for what a phone GPU would do differently from the headless SwiftShader renderer: opens the page in headless Chromium, captures every compiled shader source, and counts each program's uniform rows, varyings, samplers and attributes against the WebGL 1 minimums; then re-runs the shaders' risky maths (hashes, time arguments, far positions, derivatives) in Node with every intermediate rounded to IEEE binary16, beside fp32.
- Rules:
  - fp16: round to nearest, ties to even, 10-bit mantissa, exponents -14 to 15 with subnormals, overflow to infinity (Node 22 has no Math.f16round).
  - A case fails only for a shader declared mediump (on a phone mediump may be fp16); cases for shaders that ask for highp where present (HIP, PRE) are reported as notes, because every OpenGL ES 3 GPU has highp in fragment shaders.
  - Each case quotes the shader text it mirrors; when that text is gone from the page the case is reported STALE and the tool fails.
  - Uniform rows are counted without packing (every float, vec2 and vec3 a whole row), so a real count is at most the one shown; minimums: 128 vertex and 16 fragment uniform vectors, 8 varyings, 8 texture units, 8 attributes.
  - Writes nothing; exit 1 on a failing or stale case.
- Inputs:
  - [docklands/index.html](docklands/index.html)
  - [docklands/sky.js](docklands/sky.js)
- Outputs: none (prints only)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: check; after: nothing
- Faults: 

#### Hand step `page-assets`: Pixel-art palette and music list for the 3D page

- By: hand; date: 2026-10-03
- Method: The pixel-art palette: 24 colours extracted from an owner-supplied reference picture by PIL median cut and k-means (a one-off run; the picture is not stored), plus 9 accent colours added by hand. The music list: tracks chosen by hand from Wikimedia Commons with title, artist, licence and page.
- Rules:
  - Pixel-art colours do not follow height alone (owner, 2026-10-03: it reads as a heat map); materials carry the variety.
  - Music: CC BY 3.0 tracks (Kevin MacLeod) served by Wikimedia Commons with Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *, so the page can analyse them; the credit is shown while a track plays.
- Inputs:
  - **owner-supplied** (supplied by the repository owner, owner's)
  - **commons-music** (Music by Kevin MacLeod, CC BY 3.0 (attribution; not share-alike))
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/pixel-palette.json](docklands/data/pixel-palette.json)
  - [docklands/data/music.json](docklands/data/music.json)
- Decisions recorded in: docklands/data/pixel-palette.json; docklands/data/music.json
- Not committed as code: The palette extraction was a one-off script and is not committed.

### 17. Facades

#### 45. `facade-measure` (registry/sources/facades/tools/facade.py)

- Command: `cd magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/facades/work && python3 ../tools/facade.py <img> <out_prefix> x0 y0 x1 y1`
- Method: Rectifies a hand-chosen facade region of an open photo and measures its bay and floor periods and its colours.
- Rules:
  - OpenCV LSD segments; RANSAC vertical and horizontal vanishing points; affine rectification by homography.
  - Periods by autocorrelation of gradient profiles, FFT cross-check; the fundamental chosen by looking at the patch.
  - Colours: k-means, k = 3, in Lab.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/facades/<cwb-id>/src-<id>.jpg` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/rois.json](registry/sources/facades/work/rois.json)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/meas.json](registry/sources/facades/work/meas.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: nothing
- Hand judgement: The facade region in each photo is chosen by eye (registry/sources/facades/work/rois.json); the fundamental period is chosen by looking at the patch; the outputs are collected into meas.json by hand.
- Note: Also tools/measure.py for a patch cut by hand. The outputs were collected into meas.json by hand.

#### 46. `facade-measure-patch` (registry/sources/facades/tools/measure.py)

- Command: `cd magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/facades/work && python3 ../tools/measure.py <img> x0 y0 x1 y1 <out.jpg> [k]`
- Method: Periods and colours of a patch cut by hand from a photo, with no rectification (for faces seen square on).
- Rules:
  - Same measure and colour functions as facade.py.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/facades/<cwb-id>/src-<id>.jpg` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/meas.json](registry/sources/facades/work/meas.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: nothing
- Hand judgement: The patch is cut by hand; the outputs are collected into meas.json by hand.
- Note: Outputs collected into meas.json by hand.

#### 47. `facade-metric` (registry/sources/facades/tools/metric.py)

- Command: `cd magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/facades/work && python3 ../tools/metric.py`
- Method: Metric aspect of each rectified patch from its vanishing points and a focal length.
- Rules:
  - Focal length from the two vanishing points when plausible, else a 26 mm-equivalent phone lens.
- Inputs:
  - `data/raw/facades/<cwb-id>/src-<id>.jpg` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/rois.json](registry/sources/facades/work/rois.json)
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/meas.json](registry/sources/facades/work/meas.json)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/aspect.json](registry/sources/facades/work/aspect.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `facade-measure`

#### 48. `facade-build` (registry/sources/facades/tools/build.py)

- Command: `cd magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/facades/work && python3 ../tools/build.py`
- Method: Writes the rectified patches at metric aspect and the bays, floors and colours in metres.
- Rules:
  - Floor height = height / floors (assumed) unless a face width gives a scale; bay = OSM face width / bay count when the whole face is visible.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/rois.json](registry/sources/facades/work/rois.json)
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/aspect.json](registry/sources/facades/work/aspect.json)
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/meas.json](registry/sources/facades/work/meas.json)
  - `data/raw/facades/<cwb-id>/src-<id>.jpg` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/results.json](registry/sources/facades/work/results.json)
  - `data/raw/facades/<cwb-id>/rect-<key>.jpg` (local, not committed)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `facade-metric`
- Hand judgement: The floor count used to scale a patch is an assumed or judged value where no face width is visible.

#### 49. `facade-write` (registry/sources/facades/tools/write_facades.py)

- Command: `cd magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/facades/work && python3 ../tools/write_facades.py`
- Method: Joins the measurements, the footprint widths and the judged facts into one record per tower, each value marked measured, judged or assumed.
- Rules:
  - Judged facts and their sources are written in the script.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/results.json](registry/sources/facades/work/results.json)
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/footprints.json](registry/sources/facades/work/footprints.json)
  - [registry/sources/facades/photos.json](registry/sources/facades/photos.json)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/facades/facades.json](registry/sources/facades/facades.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `facade-build`
- Hand judgement: Judged facts (by looking at photos) and their sources are written in write_facades.py; each value is marked measured, judged or assumed in facades.json.

#### 50. `build-facade-atlas` (tools/build-facade-atlas.py)

- Command: `python3 magpie/cwplans/tools/build-facade-atlas.py`
- Method: Cuts one tile per tower from its rectified open photo, holding whole floors and whole bays so it repeats along the walls, and packs the tiles into an 8 x 4 atlas of 256 px tiles.
- Rules:
  - One patch per tower, chosen by eye from data/raw/facades/contact.jpg; Citigroup Centre left out (crane, blur).
  - Tile = whole floors (about 40% of the patch, at most 25 m) by whole bays; size in metres from the measured floor height or bay width.
  - Attribution per tile from photos.json.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/sources/facades/work/results.json](registry/sources/facades/work/results.json)
  - [registry/sources/facades/facades.json](registry/sources/facades/facades.json)
  - [registry/sources/facades/photos.json](registry/sources/facades/photos.json)
  - `data/raw/facades/<cwb-id>/rect-<key>.jpg` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [docklands/data/tex/facades.jpg](docklands/data/tex/facades.jpg)
  - [docklands/data/tex/facades.json](docklands/data/tex/facades.json)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: derive; after: `facade-build`, `facade-write`
- Hand judgement: One patch per tower chosen by eye from data/raw/facades/contact.jpg; Citigroup Centre left out (crane, blur).
