- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `walk-london-datastore`
- Hand judgement: The hand rules (HAND in the tool) choose resources where the general rules would read too much or the wrong file (per-link emission workbooks, variant zips, superseded releases) and thin the LAEI 20 m concentration grids to 100 m; each has its reason. Datasets of incident records at addresses (LFB incidents, mobilisations, animal rescues) are held with the lift entrapments for the owner. Outputs were read by hand after each batch (counts, zone keys, sizes).
- Faults: [F22](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F27](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 56. `amend-uprns` (tools/amend-uprns.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/amend-uprns.mjs cultural-infrastructure [--refresh]; generic: node magpie/cwplans/tools/amend-uprns.mjs --in <file> --uprn-field <f> --id-field <f[,f]> [--ref a:<file>]... [--ref c:<file>]... [--open-uprn <zip|csv>] [--footprints <osm-clip.json.gz>] --out <copy> --amendments <json>`
- Method: Copies a spreadsheet-sourced file and amends only the UPRN cells that a spreadsheet rounded (F22). Every cell is classified (rounded: scientific notation, or 9+ digits ending in 5+ zeros; catalogued only: empty, text, decimal, out of range, padded, duplicate across venues, not in OS Open UPRN, over 150 m from its OS Open UPRN point, and per-layer constant position offsets, F25). Each rounded venue is recovered by the first route that gives exactly one candidate inside the range the rounded value allows: (a) the same dataset in other formats, layers and releases, (b) OS Open UPRN points in the venue's OSM building outline, (c) registers with UPRNs; else (d) unknown with the reason. Writes the amended copy beside the source and an amendments file with one entry per changed cell, every unknown, the catalogue, the rules and the measured precision of routes a and b (each valid 12-digit UPRN rounded to 3 and 6 digits and recovered blind).
- Rules:
  - The source file is read, never written; a value that is not in a rounded class is never changed.
  - Range: Excel rounds to k significant digits; the true value lies within half a unit of the last kept digit (1.00023E+11 -> [100022500000, 100023500000); plain 200000000000 -> k = non-zero prefix -> [150000000000, 250000000000)); rounded forms of the same venue in other releases narrow it by intersection.
  - Venue: rows with the same normalised name within 50 m are one venue; candidates are pooled and every row gets the same answer.
  - Route a: same normalised name (or one contains the other, 8+ characters) and point within 50 m (or the same address1 without a point); exactly one distinct full UPRN in range.
  - Route b: the smallest OSM building outline containing the venue point (none: OS Open UPRN points within 10 m); in a layer with a measured constant offset the moved point is tried too; exactly one OS Open UPRN point in range.
  - Route c: as route a over GIAS, ODS and Active Places rows with UPRNs.
  - Confidence: high = route a, exact name, OS Open UPRN point of the candidate within 150 m of the venue (or of the venue moved by its layer offset); medium = other route a, route c, route b with the candidate within 1 m of the venue's own point in an outline of at most 25 UPRN points; low = other route b. High and medium applied; low recorded, not applied.
  - Two or more candidates: never chosen between; the venue stays unknown with the candidates listed.
- Inputs:
  - [feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.geojson](feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.geojson)
  - [feeds/london-datastore/catalogue.json](feeds/london-datastore/catalogue.json)
  - **lds-23697** (London Datastore 23697: Cultural Infrastructure Map 2023, OGL v3.0): https://data.london.gov.uk/download/23697/<resource id>/<file>; GET every CSV, zip and venue GeoPackage of the dataset (all revisions; CEZ files skipped)
  - **lds-2ko88** (London Datastore 2ko88: Cultural Infrastructure Map, OGL v3.0): https://data.london.gov.uk/download/2ko88/<resource id>/<file>; GET every CSV, zip and venue GeoPackage (the 2018-2020 releases)
  - **lds-2zj1y** (London Datastore 2zj1y: Cultural Infrastructure Map 2024, OGL v3.0): https://data.london.gov.uk/download/2zj1y/<resource id>/<file>; GET the 2024 LGBTQ+ venues CSV
  - **gla-cim-arcgis** (GLA ArcGIS map service behind the public Cultural Infrastructure Map app, not stated on the service; it serves the 2023 Cultural Infrastructure Map that the London Datastore publishes under OGL v3.0 (lds-23697)): https://gis.london.gov.uk/arcgis/rest/services/apps/Cultural_infrastructure_2023_for_webapp_verified/MapServer/<layer>/query; GET each of 37 layers, zone envelope in EPSG:27700, outFields=*, outSR=4326, f=geojson, pages of 1,000
  - `data/raw/registry/osopenuprn_202609_csv.zip` (local, not committed)
  - `data/raw/docklands/osm-clip.json.gz` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/gias.json](registry/sources/registers/gias.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/ods.json](registry/sources/registers/ods.json)
  - [registry/sources/registers/active-places.json](registry/sources/registers/active-places.json)
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.uprn-amended.geojson](feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.uprn-amended.geojson)
  - [feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.uprn-amendments.json](feeds/london-datastore/cultural-infrastructure/cultural-infrastructure.uprn-amendments.json)
  - `data/raw/uprn-amend/` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: transform; after: `walk-london-datastore`
- Hand judgement: The thresholds (50 m, 150 m, 10 m, 1 m, 25 UPRN points) and the confidence levels are hand choices, recorded in the tool header constants and in skills/cwplans-london-datastore/SKILL.md "Amending rounded UPRNs"; the measured precision that supports them is in the amendments file meta.validation. The 2025 Cultural Infrastructure Map (2rj5o, no licence on the Datastore) was left out by decision.
- Faults: [F22](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F25](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 61. `check-feeds` (feeds/check-feeds.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/feeds/check-feeds.mjs [source ids] [--json] [--live]`
- Method: Re-checks every catalogued feed and prints its HTTP status and CORS answer, and where it differs from feeds.json.
- Rules:
  - An api is fetched only when the source has no sample; CORS counts when Access-Control-Allow-Origin is * or the site origin.
  - Network: 6 workers, but at most one request at a time to a host and 1 s between requests to the same host, 30 s timeout, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1, Origin https://danbri.github.io on api and sample requests; only the first chunk of each body is read. Nothing is written.
- Inputs:
  - [feeds/feeds.json](feeds/feeds.json)
  - **feed-providers** (the publishers of the feeds and APIs listed in feeds/feeds.json and feeds/events.json, per entry in feeds.json): every url, api and sample URL in feeds/feeds.json; HTTP GET with Origin https://danbri.github.io; status, content type, CORS
- Outputs: none (prints only)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: check; after: nothing
- Faults: [F11](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 62. `check-events` (feeds/check-events.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/feeds/check-events.mjs [source ids] [--json] [--live] [--url URL]`
- Method: Re-checks the event and real-time feeds and prints status, CORS, item count, newest item date and the first titles.
- Rules:
  - Item counts come from generic parsers and can differ from the hand-tuned samples in events.json.
  - Network: 6 workers, but at most one request at a time to a host and 1 s between requests to the same host, 30 s timeout, User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; a second request with the Origin header tests CORS. Nothing is written.
- Inputs:
  - [feeds/events.json](feeds/events.json)
  - **feed-providers** (the publishers of the feeds and APIs listed in feeds/feeds.json and feeds/events.json, per entry in feeds.json): every event and real-time feed URL in feeds/events.json; HTTP GET, with and without the Origin header; items and newest date by small generic parsers (ICS, RSS, Atom, JSON, schema.org Event JSON-LD)
- Outputs: none (prints only)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: check; after: nothing
- Faults: [F11](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### 63. `fetch-live` (tools/fetch-live.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-live.mjs [bikes lifts crowding jamcams ukpn overflows notams helicopters] [--no-fetch]`
- Method: Fetches live and fast-changing state for the Docklands zone (the model box plus the east margin of fetch-works) and writes one dated snapshot per source with a common item shape {id, kind, name, zone, time, position, values, url}: Santander Cycles docks, TfL lift outages, live station busyness, JamCam cameras, UKPN power cuts, Thames Water storm overflows, NOTAMs (cranes and temporary areas) and the UK AIP helicopter structure (H4 and restricted areas).
- Rules:
  - Zone: BOX_WGS84 of fetch-docklands.mjs (-0.095, 51.474, 0.015, 51.522) = 'model'; 0.015 to 0.085 E, 51.495 to 51.522 N = 'east'; items outside are dropped, except context items in helicopters.json (zone null).
  - Bikes: values as published (NbBikes, NbStandardBikes, NbEBikes, NbEmptyDocks, NbDocks; Installed, Locked, Temporary); unavailable_docks = NbDocks - NbBikes - NbEmptyDocks; time = the newest property 'modified'.
  - Lifts: stationUniqueId matched to StopPoint naptanId, then hubNaptanCode; kept when the station is in the zone; message kept as published.
  - Crowding: Tube and DLR (940GZZLU, 940GZZDL) then 910G station naptans in the zone, one at a time; percentageOfBaseline rounded to 0.001; dataAvailable false and 404 counted, not listed.
  - JamCams: position, view, available, imageUrl and videoUrl recorded; no image fetched or stored.
  - UKPN: kept when geopoint is in the zone; fullpostcodedata, mainmessage, plannedincidentreason and incidentcategorycustomerfriendlydescription dropped.
  - Storm overflows: Status 1 discharging (indicated, not confirmed), 0 not, -1 monitor offline; epoch milliseconds to UTC ISO.
  - NOTAMs: kept when a position stated in item E (DDMMSS(.s)N DDDMMSS(.s)E/W) lies in the zone, or item E states none and the Q-line centre does with a radius of at most 5 NM; deduplicated by series/number/year; kept: Q-code, FIR, item A, validity (PERM kept as text), lower/upper FL, Q radius, item E positions and 'n FT AGL / m FT AMSL' heights, area vertices for 'BOUNDED BY', the first bracketed place label; dropped: item E text, phone numbers, contacts.
  - Helicopters: H4 rows of EGLL AD 2.22 para 12 (the table anchored on 'Isle-of-Dogs'): reporting type (compulsory or on request), name, OS grid reference, position, description cut to 120 characters; the H4 vertical profile as printed; ENR 5.1 EGR158, EGR159, EGR160: vertices, arc centres and radii, upper limit, permitted users, SI; an area is in the zone when its vertex centroid or a vertex is.
  - Network: User-Agent glitchcan-cwplans/0.1; one request at a time per host, at least 1.5 s apart; up to 5 tries with Retry-After or a doubling pause on 429, 5xx, timeouts and resets; 404 returned without retry; files overwritten on every run.
- Inputs:
  - **tfl-live-feeds** (Transport for London Unified API live feeds: BikePoint, TfL open data terms (Transport Data Service; OGL-based, attribution required); the terms page refuses scripted requests: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/BikePoint; every dock, one request
  - **tfl-live-feeds** (Transport for London Unified API live feeds: BikePoint, TfL open data terms (Transport Data Service; OGL-based, attribution required); the terms page refuses scripted requests: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/Disruptions/Lifts/v2/; every current lift outage, one request
  - **tfl-live-feeds** (Transport for London Unified API live feeds: BikePoint, TfL open data terms (Transport Data Service; OGL-based, attribution required); the terms page refuses scripted requests: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/StopPoint/Mode/tube,dlr,elizabeth-line,overground; station positions, naptan and hub codes; cached (reused when present)
  - **tfl-live-feeds** (Transport for London Unified API live feeds: BikePoint, TfL open data terms (Transport Data Service; OGL-based, attribution required); the terms page refuses scripted requests: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/crowding/{naptan}/Live; one request per Tube, DLR, Elizabeth line and Overground station naptan in the zone (65 on 2026-10-04)
  - **tfl-live-feeds** (Transport for London Unified API live feeds: BikePoint, TfL open data terms (Transport Data Service; OGL-based, attribution required); the terms page refuses scripted requests: re-check before production): https://api.tfl.gov.uk/Place/Type/JamCam; every camera, one request
  - **ukpn-live-faults** (UK Power Networks Open Data Portal, dataset ukpn-live-faults, CC BY 4.0 (dataset metadata, read 2026-10-04)): https://ukpowernetworks.opendatasoft.com/api/explore/v2.1/catalog/datasets/ukpn-live-faults/exports/json; the whole dataset
  - **thames-water-storm-overflows** (Thames Water Storm Overflow Activity, CC BY 4.0 (item licenceInfo, read 2026-10-04)): https://services2.arcgis.com/g6o32ZDQ33GpCIu3/arcgis/rest/services/Thames_Water_Storm_Overflow_Activity_(Production)_view/FeatureServer/0/query; envelope -0.095,51.474,0.085,51.522 (inSR 4326), outFields=*, returnGeometry=false
  - **nats-pib** (NATS UK AIS contingency full UK PIB, © NATS Limited; no open licence stated. Facts only (NOTAM id, Q-line code, positions, heights, validity, area vertices, short place label); no NOTAM text, contacts or phone numbers kept. Re-check before production): https://pibs.nats.co.uk/operational/pibs/PIB.xml; the full UK PIB, one request
  - **nats-uk-aip** (UK AIP, Crown copyright / NATS; no open licence stated. Facts only (names, grid references, positions, altitudes, limits); no tables, charts or text copied. Re-check against the current issue before production): https://www.aurora.nats.co.uk/htmlAIP/Publications/2024-03-21-AIRAC/html/eAIP/EG-AD-2.EGLL-en-GB.html and EG-ENR-5.1-en-GB.html; two pages; cached (reused when present)
  - `data/raw/live/ (raw responses; --no-fetch reads them)` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [feeds/live/bikes.json](feeds/live/bikes.json)
  - [feeds/live/lifts.json](feeds/live/lifts.json)
  - [feeds/live/crowding.json](feeds/live/crowding.json)
  - [feeds/live/jamcams.json](feeds/live/jamcams.json)
  - [feeds/live/ukpn.json](feeds/live/ukpn.json)
  - [feeds/live/overflows.json](feeds/live/overflows.json)
  - [feeds/live/notams.json](feeds/live/notams.json)
  - [feeds/live/helicopters.json](feeds/live/helicopters.json)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: nothing
- Hand judgement: Which sources are open enough to commit (TfL terms, CC BY 4.0) and which only as facts (NATS PIB, UK AIP) or not at all (CAA CAP 1455; OpenSky, adsb.fi, airplanes.live, ADS-B Exchange; adsb.lol as ODbL without the owner's agreement) is recorded in data-register.json sources, feeds/live/README.md and the skill cwplans-live-state.

#### 69. `walk-portals` (tools/walk-portals.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/walk-portals.mjs <dgu|pdg|boroughs|nomis|onsgeo|national> walk [--refresh]; node magpie/cwplans/tools/walk-portals.mjs <portal> triage; NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/walk-portals.mjs <portal> harvest [key ...]; node magpie/cwplans/tools/walk-portals.mjs dgu catalogue; node magpie/cwplans/tools/walk-portals.mjs index`
- Method: Walks the open-data portals other than the London Datastore that cover the zone, one adapter per portal (tools/portals/*.mjs): reads each catalogue whole (data.gov.uk CKAN: all 59,451 datasets; planning.data.gov.uk: all 222 datasets with a count of the entities that meet the zone), triages every dataset in scope by written rules to one final state (harvested, held, listed-for-harvest, deferred, not-relevant, not-open, unavailable, sensitive, walked-elsewhere), and harvests the open, relevant datasets clipped to the 3D model box as GeoJSON (WGS84) or JSON rows with a meta member (source, API, fetch date, licence, attribution, method, counts).
- Rules:
  - Politeness: one request at a time over the run, at least 1.1 s apart, robots.txt read for every host before the first request and obeyed (longest matching rule), backoff on 429 and 5xx with Retry-After, the project User-Agent.
  - Licence class (licenceClass in walk-portals.mjs): OGL, CC BY, ODC-By, CC0/PDDL/public domain are open; NC or closed is restricted; CC BY-SA and ODbL are share-alike (never harvested; OSM is the only share-alike exception); 'no conditions apply' alone is open-unclear; no licence is none (metadata only). data.gov.uk records with no licence whose resources all sit on GOV.UK, ONS, Nomis, NHS Digital, DfT road traffic or data.police.uk count as ogl-site-terms (those sites' terms give OGL v3.0).
  - data.gov.uk scope: S1 local publishers (zone boroughs, LTGDC, Canal & River Trust, TfL, Royal Museums Greenwich, PLA, local NHS), S2 named national publishers, S3 the GLA (the London Datastore: state walked-elsewhere), S4 other publishers whose title or tags name a zone place or borough; out of scope only counted per organisation.
  - data.gov.uk states, first match: T0 by hand; T1 sensitive title; T2 held (dataset id, or a name of 15+ characters with a hyphen, in a tool, data-register.json or pipeline.json); T3 walked-elsewhere (GLA); T4 unavailable (no resources); T5 not-relevant administrative (payments, spending, staff, contracts, FOI); T6 not-open; T7 not-relevant other area (bounding box misses the zone, or the title names another region, county, city or London borough); T8 deferred documents only; T9 listed-for-harvest (zone place, zone borough, London-fine or national-fine); T10 not-relevant coarser than a borough; T11 deferred area unknown.
  - Zone place names (title and tags only, not the description): the London Datastore list plus Thames Barrier, Tideway, Lower Lea, Leamouth; not when the text has tree or crop words (Poplar is also a tree: 'Energy Crops Scheme' and short rotation coppice records matched it).
  - planning.data.gov.uk: zone = entities whose geometry intersects the box polygon (the platform's spatial query); for documents, agreements and plans the entities of the zone planning organisations (GLA 144, Greenwich 150, Lewisham 198, City 203, Newham 246, Southwark 329, Tower Hamlets 350). States P0 by hand (held from another source), P1 harvested, P2 ended, P3 not-open, P4 empty, P5 code list, P6 nothing in the zone, P7 listed-for-harvest, P8 deferred.
  - Harvest: whole geometries that meet the box are kept, rounded to 6 decimals, with in_zone, whole_in_zone, in_cw; EA flood zones are simplified (0.5 m) and cut at the box (clipped_to_zone). Fields matching e-mail, phone, contact, owner name, person, officer, agent, applicant are dropped and listed.
  - Committed outputs stay small: the data.gov.uk triage lists datasets one by one only for the states that need a decision or hold data; the bulk states are name lists keyed by state, rule and reason. National files stay raw and uncommitted; only zone extracts are committed.
- Inputs:
  - **op-dgu-catalogue** (data.gov.uk catalogue, catalogue metadata OGL v3.0 (data.gov.uk); each dataset has its own licence, recorded per record): https://www.data.gov.uk/api/action/package_search; walk: GET ?rows=1000&start=<n>&sort=metadata_created asc (60 pages, 59,451 datasets on 2026-10-04)
  - **op-pdg-catalogue** (planning.data.gov.uk, OGL v3.0 (every dataset; attribution text per dataset)): https://www.planning.data.gov.uk/dataset.json; walk: GET dataset.json, then one entity.json count per dataset (zone polygon, intersects; or per zone planning organisation)
  - **op-pdg-agricultural-land-classification** (planning.data.gov.uk: Agricultural land classification, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.planning.data.gov.uk/entity.geojson?dataset=agricultural-land-classification&geometry=<zone polygon WKT>&geometry_relation=intersects&limit=500&offset=<n>; pdg harvest agricultural-land-classification
  - **op-pdg-air-quality-management-area** (planning.data.gov.uk: Air quality management area, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.planning.data.gov.uk/entity.geojson?dataset=air-quality-management-area&geometry=<zone polygon WKT>&geometry_relation=intersects&limit=500&offset=<n>; pdg harvest air-quality-management-area
  - **op-pdg-article-4-direction** (planning.data.gov.uk: Article 4 direction, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.planning.data.gov.uk/entity.json?dataset=article-4-direction&organisation_entity=<198|203|329|350>&limit=500&offset=<n>; pdg harvest article-4-direction
  - **op-pdg-article-4-direction-area** (planning.data.gov.uk: Article 4 direction area, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.planning.data.gov.uk/entity.geojson?dataset=article-4-direction-area&geometry=<zone polygon WKT>&geometry_relation=intersects&limit=500&offset=<n>; pdg harvest article-4-direction-area
  - **op-pdg-certificate-of-immunity** (planning.data.gov.uk: Certificate of immunity, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.planning.data.gov.uk/entity.geojson?dataset=certificate-of-immunity&geometry=<zone polygon WKT>&geometry_relation=intersects&limit=500&offset=<n>; pdg harvest certificate-of-immunity
  - **op-pdg-community-infrastructure-levy-schedule** (planning.data.gov.uk: Community infrastructure levy schedule, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.planning.data.gov.uk/entity.json?dataset=community-infrastructure-levy-schedule&organisation_entity=<144|203>&limit=500&offset=<n>; pdg harvest community-infrastructure-levy-schedule
  - **op-pdg-conservation-area-document** (planning.data.gov.uk: Conservation area document, Open Government Licence v3.0): https://www.planning.data.gov.uk/entity.json?dataset=conservation-area-document&organisation_entity=<150|198|246|350>&limit=500&offset=<n>; pdg harvest conservation-area-document

#### 88. `discover-feeds` (tools/discover-feeds.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/discover-feeds.mjs [seed|cc|autodiscover|verify|build] [--refresh] [--no-merge] (cc needs CC_LIBS=<dir with hyparquet>)`
- Method: Finds London RSS, Atom, JSON Feed and iCalendar feeds by 18 methods (directories, GitHub lists, Wikipedia/Wikidata, the ICNN map, hand lists, ModernGov, council and public-body pages, the GLA feed list, Mastodon, Bluesky, Lemmy, groups.io search, Meetup, the earlier site crawl, Common Crawl), autodiscovers feeds on home pages, fetches and parses every feed, counts items, the newest date, CORS and zone-place mentions, and writes the candidate list, an OPML of live feeds, and the verified zone feeds into feeds/events.json.
- Rules:
  - Autodiscovery: <link rel="alternate"> RSS, Atom and JSON Feed links (comment feeds dropped) and up to three .ics/webcal links; a hand-listed page with none is tried at /feed/, /rss.xml, /feed.xml, /rss, /news/rss/ until one parses; at most three feeds per page.
  - Status: verified (parses with at least one item), empty, not a feed, http error, network error, blocked (Cloudflare, SiteGround, Imperva, Azure WAF pages), robots disallowed, robots unreachable (5xx or no answer = disallow), not fetched (terms). live = verified with an item dated within 365 days.
  - Relevance, counts only: zone_items = items whose title, summary, content or categories name a zone place (Canary Wharf, Isle of Dogs, Docklands, Millwall, Cubitt Town, Blackwall, Poplar, Limehouse, Wapping, Shadwell, Rotherhithe, Surrey Quays, Canada Water, Deptford, Greenwich, Silvertown, Royal Docks and others) or district E14, SE16, SE10, SE8, E16, E1W; recent = dated within 120 days; score = 3 x zone_recent + zone + 5 x share. No item text stored; at most two titles cut to 80 characters, none for social, forum and mailing-list feeds.
  - Duplicates: key = host without www + path without trailing slash + query; Blogger /atom.xml, /rss.xml and /feeds/posts/default are one feed; utm_ parameters dropped.
  - Bluesky: a handle is kept only when it is a registrable domain under .gov.uk, .nhs.uk, .police.uk, .ac.uk, .sch.uk or .london, or the domain of a publisher already found (private people excluded). groups.io: London groups only (name or description places them in London); feeds not fetched (robots.txt). Reddit: not fetched (robots.txt Disallow: /).
  - Common Crawl CC-MAIN-2026-39: hosts ending .london (all) and .uk hosts whose name holds a London place; sample by sha1(host): .london 200, zone places all (cap 500), boroughs 100, "london" 200; first capture with HTTP 200 and HTML; feeds kept when the page says London or has a London postcode (.london always).
  - events.json merge: re-read before writing, append only, ids disc-<slug>, layout kept (2 spaces); a live candidate not already in events.json or feeds.json, with a zone item, a zone publisher, or a council or transport body; social only with zone items; harvest: false unless kind is events (tools/fetch-works.mjs harvests verified event feeds).
  - Wikipedia: MediaWiki Action API used per its etiquette (descriptive User-Agent, one call at a time); its robots.txt Disallow: /w/ is for page crawlers and is not applied to API calls. Titles only (text is CC BY-SA).
  - Excluded, never fetched: Facebook, Nextdoor, WhatsApp, X, Instagram, TikTok (no feed, terms forbid scraping), Google Groups (feeds removed), JISCMail (Cloudflare challenge), Meetup RSS (robots.txt), Eventbrite (token), Google News RSS (terms).
- Inputs:
  - [registry/sources/web/discovered-feeds.json](registry/sources/web/discovered-feeds.json)
  - [feeds/events.json](feeds/events.json)
  - [feeds/feeds.json](feeds/feeds.json)
  - `data/raw/feed-discovery/` (local, not committed)
  - **feed-directories** (feed directories and lists read for London feed URLs: ooh.directory OPML exports, facts only: feed URLs, names and websites; Feedspot pages are copyright Feedspot, the GitHub files have no licence file, Wikipedia text is CC BY-SA (titles only kept), Wikidata is CC0): https://ooh.directory/feeds/cats/<id>/opml/<name>.xml; https://rss.feedspot.com/<slug>/; https://raw.githubusercontent.com/<repo>/HEAD/<path>; https://www.google.com/maps/d/kml?mid=<ICNN map>&forcekml=1; https://en.wikipedia.org/w/api.php (categorymembers, links, pageprops); QLever or WDQS (P856, P576); one GET per list page; 22 Feedspot slugs tried (13 exist); one SPARQL query with VALUES over the QIDs
  - **common-crawl-index** (Common Crawl CC-MAIN-2026-39: columnar URL index, Common Crawl terms of use; pages remain their publishers' copyright: only feed URLs, page URLs and counts kept): https://data.commoncrawl.org/crawl-data/CC-MAIN-2026-39/cc-index-table.paths.gz, cc-index/table/cc-main/warc/crawl=CC-MAIN-2026-39/subset=warc/*.parquet, crawl-data/.../*.warc.gz; HTTP range requests, one at a time, >= 1 s apart, up to 4 tries on 403/429/5xx (15, 45, 135 s)
  - **social-feeds** (public social and forum feeds: Mastodon hashtag RSS, posts belong to their authors under each platform's terms: only feed URLs, item counts and zone-mention counts kept, no post text or titles): https://<instance>/tags/<tag>.rss; https://public.api.bsky.app/xrpc/app.bsky.actor.searchActors; https://bsky.app/profile/<handle>/rss; https://feddit.uk/api/v3/search, /feeds/c/<name>.xml; https://groups.io/search?q=<term>; GET; groups.io and Reddit feeds never fetched
  - **feed-providers** (the publishers of the feeds and APIs listed in feeds/feeds.json and feeds/events.json, per entry in feeds.json): every candidate feed URL and every hand-listed or discovered home page; robots.txt (RFC 9309) on every host and redirect; one request at a time per host >= 1 s apart; 8 hosts at once; 30 s timeout; up to 3 tries on 429/503; a second request with Origin https://danbri.github.io for CORS
- Outputs:
  - [feeds/discovery/candidates.json](feeds/discovery/candidates.json)
  - [feeds/discovery/london-feeds.opml](feeds/discovery/london-feeds.opml)
  - [feeds/events.json](feeds/events.json)
  - `data/raw/feed-discovery/{seeds,cc-feeds,cc-footers-<crawl>,autodiscover,verify,robots}.json` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: fetch; after: `crawl-sites`
- Hand judgement: The hand lists (local news, community, councils, public bodies, hashtags, subreddits), the Feedspot slugs, the Common Crawl sample sizes, the kind of each seed and the events.json merge rule are hand decisions written in the tool; recorded in skills/cwplans-feed-discovery/SKILL.md.
- Faults: [F11](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### Hand step `feeds-catalogue`: Catalogue of live feeds, APIs and event sources

- By: research agents, merged by hand; date: 2026-10-03
- Method: Research agents surveyed data sources in seven areas (341 added on 2026-10-03, 135 left out with reasons) and event, calendar, news and openings sources (124); each URL was fetched from the container and its status, CORS, licence and a sample recorded; check-feeds.mjs and check-events.mjs re-check them.
- Rules:
  - Only official, published or openly licensed sources, and only cameras their owners publish for public viewing.
  - Left out with the reason: directories of unsecured private cameras, people-search and face-recognition sites, personal licence registers that list named individuals.
  - A source behind a bot challenge is "not verified", never "verified" (F11).
- Inputs:
  - **feed-providers** (the publishers of the feeds and APIs listed in feeds/feeds.json and feeds/events.json, per entry in feeds.json)
- Outputs:
  - [feeds/feeds.json](feeds/feeds.json)
  - [feeds/events.json](feeds/events.json)
  - [feeds/README.md](feeds/README.md)
  - [feeds/EVENTS.md](feeds/EVENTS.md)
  - [feeds/SURVEY-2026-10-03.md](feeds/SURVEY-2026-10-03.md)
- Decisions recorded in: feeds/feeds.json and feeds/events.json (status, licence, excluded section); feeds/SURVEY-2026-10-03.md
- Not committed as code: The generators of feeds/README.md and feeds/EVENTS.md are not committed ("generated and by hand").
- Faults: [F11](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

### 23. Construction sites (works in progress)

#### 67. `build-construction-index` (tools/build-construction-index.mjs)

- Command: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/build-construction-index.mjs [--no-fetch] [--refresh]`
- Method: Builds the index of works in progress: PLD records grouped into sites, a status per site from the LDD commencement and completion dates and the decision, and joins by position to NOTAM cranes, Street Manager highway activities, OSM construction areas, the brownfield register, site allocations and Wikidata, each with a confidence; hand-written facts (developer pages, the owner's photos) joined by planning reference.
- Rules:
  - Zone: model box WGS84 -0.095, 51.474 to 0.015, 51.522 plus the east margin 0.015 to 0.085, 51.495 to 51.522 (as fetch-works.mjs); the PLD centroid decides.
  - Grouping (union-find): same reference base (suffixes /A1, /S, /NC, /P1 ... removed), a description or relied-on permission that cites another record's reference within 300 m, or centroids within 10 m.
  - Status per group: S1 LDD commencement within 7 years, no completion (high); S1b status Commenced without a date, decided within 7 years (medium); S2 commenced more than 7 years ago, never completed (low, F32); S2b S2 with an overlapping OSM construction area, a NOTAM crane within 60 m or a Street Manager crane, hoarding or compound within 50 m -> on site (medium); S3 completion within 2 years and not before the latest commencement (high); S4 approved within 5 years, not lapsed (lapsed_date in the past) or superseded, no commencement (medium); S4b S4 with a NOTAM crane within 60 m or an overlapping OSM building=construction -> on site (medium, F33); S5 undecided application or appeal (high). Completed more than 2 years ago, refused, withdrawn, lapsed: counted, not listed. A commenced record is never dropped for a past lapsed_date (F33).
  - Footprint: PLD wgs84_polygon of 50 m2 or more (smaller = point marker, F35); else the smallest overlapping OSM building=construction outline, else landuse=construction; else a circle of the stated site area; else 25 m round the centroid. footprint.from says which.
  - Matches: M1 NOTAM crane to the nearest on-site, approved or stale site within 150 m (high within 60 m); M2 Street Manager activity within 120 m (high within 50 m); M3 OSM construction feature within 30 m (high when overlapping); M4 brownfield point within 40 m (geox/geoy WGS84 or BNG per row, F23); M5 site centre in a site allocation polygon; M6 Wikidata item within 40 m (high within 15 m with a shared label word, medium with the word, low without).
  - Kept per source: ids as published (PLD id and lpa_app_no, NOTAM id, Street Manager activity id and USRN, OSM type/id, brownfield site reference, allocation OBJECTID, QID), dates, and the snapshot or fetch date. PLD descriptions cut to 300 characters; no NOTAM text; OSM tags limited to name, landuse, building, construction, dates, operator, developer, levels, height, description, website, ref, address.
  - Heights: PLD building_details max_height as entered (m AOD where the description says so); storeys max of the lead record's buildings, else of the group; developer and CTBUH figures only in approved.web from facts.json.
  - Output: one site per line; borough names without "London Borough of".
- Inputs:
  - **planning-london-datahub** (Planning London Datahub, GLA Planning London Datahub terms (not confirmed as OGL): application metadata and links only): https://planningdata.london.gov.uk/api-guest/applications/_search; one POST: size 10000, centroid in the zone box (model box plus the Royal Docks margin) AND (proposed residential units >= 10 OR non-residential GIA gained >= 1000 m2); fields listed in PLD_FIELDS; cached in data/raw/construction/pld-major.json
  - **wikidata** (Wikidata, CC0 1.0): https://qlever.dev/api/wikidata; one query (lib.mjs qlever, paced): items with P625 in the zone box that are buildings (P31/P279* Q41176), construction projects (Q811430) or under construction (P5817 Q12377751), with label, P5817, P571, P1619, P2048, P1101, P193, P127, P88, P84 and their labels; cached in data/raw/construction/wikidata-buildings.json
  - **osm** (OpenStreetMap, ODbL 1.0): data/raw/docklands/greater_london-latest.osm.pbf (local); landuse=construction, building=construction, building:part=construction, construction=* (not highway or railway), disused:building=construction: nodes, ways, relation outer rings; cached in data/raw/construction/osm-construction.json
  - [feeds/live/notams.json](feeds/live/notams.json)
  - [feeds/works/street-manager-activities.json](feeds/works/street-manager-activities.json)
  - [feeds/london-datastore/brownfield-register/brownfield-register.geojson](feeds/london-datastore/brownfield-register/brownfield-register.geojson)
  - [feeds/london-datastore/site-allocations/site-allocations.geojson](feeds/london-datastore/site-allocations/site-allocations.geojson)
  - [registry/sources/construction/facts.json](registry/sources/construction/facts.json)
  - [data-register.json](data-register.json)
  - `data/raw/uk_os_OSTN15_NTv2_OSGBtoETRS.tif` (local, not committed)
- Outputs:
  - [registry/sources/construction/sites.json](registry/sources/construction/sites.json)
  - `data/raw/construction/` (local, not committed)
- Network: yes; deterministic: no; kind: derive; after: `fetch-live`, `fetch-works`, `walk-london-datastore`
- Hand judgement: The status windows (7, 5 and 2 years), the "major" filter and the match distances are judgements, recorded in the tool header, meta.status_rules and meta.match_rules and the cwplans-construction skill. facts.json is hand-written from crawled pages and the owner's photos (crawl list inside it).
- Faults: [F23](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F32](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F33](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F34](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F35](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

### 24. Quality audit

#### 87. `audit-quality` (tools/audit-quality.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/audit-quality.mjs`
- Method: Runs 39 data-quality checks over every Canary Wharf source (identity, spatial, attribute, validity, parsing, network, timeliness, coverage, semantics) and writes one record per issue with counts, rates and examples, and a generated Markdown catalogue.
- Rules:
  - Checks ID-1 to ID-5, SP-1 to SP-7, AT-1 to AT-5, VA-1 to VA-4, PL-1, PL-2, NET-1 to NET-5, TM-1 to TM-4, CV-1 to CV-4, SE-1 to SE-3.
  - The audit date is fixed in the code (2026-10-03): FSA ratings before 2023-10-03 are 'older than 3 years'; CWG copy ages are counted to that date.
  - VA-2 issues are the level tokens tools/osm-values.mjs cannot read (osmLevelsUnread).
  - Each issue has a severity (high, medium, low) and an entity (building, postcode, OSM element, FSA premises and others).
  - SP-2 leaves out occupants placed by a key (placed_confidence: SP-7). SP-6 counts an outline as the mall when its name, OSM name or addr:housename names it. CV-2 counts a CWG entry as seen by another source when a registry occupant from another source carries its URL after the registry join. CV-3: CWG entries the registry could not place, by reason.
  - CV-4: register records in the registry box with no building, by reason; ID-5: register UPRNs not used (shared across postcodes, or far from the register point); SE-3: register addresses that are not where the organisation works.
- Inputs:
  - [registry/buildings.json](registry/buildings.json)
  - [atlas/data/atlas.json](atlas/data/atlas.json)
  - [postcodes/postcodes.json](postcodes/postcodes.json)
  - [data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz](data/raw/registry/osm-cw.json.gz)
  - [data/raw/registry/wikidata-cw.json](data/raw/registry/wikidata-cw.json)
  - [data/raw/registry/fhrs/FHRS530-2026-10-03.json.gz](data/raw/registry/fhrs/FHRS530-2026-10-03.json.gz)
  - [registry/sources/brands/branches.json](registry/sources/brands/branches.json)
  - [registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json](registry/sources/brands/cwg-directory.json)
  - [registry/companies-by-postcode.json](registry/companies-by-postcode.json)
  - [data/sourced-levels.json](data/sourced-levels.json)
  - [docklands/data/area.js](docklands/data/area.js)
  - [docklands/data/indoor.js](docklands/data/indoor.js)
  - `data/raw/registry/wikidata-occupant-classes.json` (local, not committed)
  - [registry/sources/museums/records-open.json](registry/sources/museums/records-open.json)
- Outputs:
  - [quality/issues.json](quality/issues.json)
  - [quality/CATALOGUE.md](quality/CATALOGUE.md)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: audit; after: `build-registry`, `build-atlas`, `build-categories`, `postcodes-build`, `registry-osm`, `registry-wikidata`, `registry-fhrs`, `build-branches`, `fetch-cwg`, `build-docklands`, `build-indoor`
- Hand judgement: Each new check is read by hand against its examples before it is trusted (curation skill, "Catalogue first").
- Faults: [F1](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F2](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F3](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F4](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F7](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F8](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F9](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F12](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F13](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F16](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F17](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F18](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F19](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register), [F20](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register)

#### Hand step `quality-analysis`: Analysis of the error classes and the fault register

- By: hand; date: 2026-10-03
- Method: Reads the audit output, names the error class of each wrong value, writes the analysis and the proposed compositing layers, and records faults in our own joins and tools in the fault register with the measured effect of each fix.
- Rules:
  - Catalogue first: name the class, add a check if none covers it; do not edit the record (owner, October 2026).
  - Change a tool only for a pipeline or join-rule fault; re-run the audit and compare the counts.
- Inputs:
  - [quality/issues.json](quality/issues.json)
- Outputs:
  - [quality/README.md](quality/README.md)
- Decisions recorded in: quality/README.md; skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md (Fault register); skills/docklands-data-curation/ACTIVITY-LOG.md

The checks (39, run 2026-10-03; counts and examples in [quality/CATALOGUE.md](quality/CATALOGUE.md)):

| check | class | title | method | issues |
|---|---|---|---|---:|
| ID-1 | identity | One Wikidata item on more than one building | Group registry buildings by their Wikidata item. | 6 |
| ID-2 | identity | Same occupant recorded twice in one building | Within each building, occupants whose normalised names are equal (case, punctuation, "(n)" branch numbers, "Ltd", "the" removed). Split by whether the two records come from different sources (not merged) or the same source (mapped twice). | 89 |
| ID-3 | identity | Building named after an occupant or owner | OSM name of the outline equals (after normalisation) the name or brand of an occupant, or the owner. Medium when Wikidata names the building differently or the building has several occupants; low for a single-use building (a pub, a hotel), where the name may be right. | 25 |
| ID-4 | identity | Item and outline describe different things (granularity) | Wikidata height is more than twice the measured height of the outline it is attached to (LiDAR or OSM): the item is a tower, the outline a podium or a part. | 0 |
| SP-1 | position | Named OSM occupant outside every building outline | Named OSM shops, offices, amenities, leisure, healthcare, tourism and craft features in the box that the registry did not place in or within 12 m of an outline. | 161 |
| SP-2 | position | Occupant placed by proximity, not containment | Occupants with placed = "near" (point outside the outline but within 12 m), against all placed occupants. | 173 |
| SP-3 | position | FSA position is the postcode centre, or shared by several premises | FSA premises in E14 with a position: within 3 m of the ONSPD point for their own postcode, or at exactly the same coordinate as two or more other premises. | 412 |
| SP-4 | position | Same branch placed far apart by two sources | Branches matched to both an OSM feature and an FSA record that both have positions: distance between them. | 30 |
| SP-5 | position | Postcode far from the feature that carries it | OSM features with addr:postcode and FSA premises with a position: distance to the ONSPD point of that postcode. Flagged above 300 m. | 43 |
| SP-6 | position | Mall or below-ground occupant placed by a 2D footprint | Occupants in a mall (CWG) or below ground (OSM level under 0, or a CWG "Mall Level -1"/"Lower Mall" level) that the registry placed in an outline which is not that mall (by its name, OSM name or OSM addr:housename) and has no recorded floor below ground. The Canary Wharf malls run under several building footprints, so a point-in-outline test assigns their shops to whichever building is above. | 122 |
| AT-1 | attribute conflict | Building heights disagree between sources | Pairs of OSM height tag, Wikidata height and LiDAR height (only where the 3D model took the height from LiDAR) that differ by more than 10 m and 10%. ID-4 cases (granularity) are left out. | 12 |
| AT-2 | attribute conflict | Floor counts disagree, or do not fit the height | OSM building:levels against Wikidata floors above ground (difference of 2 or more); and height divided by floors outside 2.5 to 6 m. | 17 |
| AT-3 | attribute conflict | OSM level and Canary Wharf Group mall level disagree | Occupants with both an OSM level tag and a CWG level ("Mall Level -1", "Street Level 0"): the offset CWG minus OSM, per mall. | 59 |
| AT-4 | attribute conflict | Category schemes do not line up (crosswalk) | For branches found in both OSM and the FSA file: pairs of OSM tag and FSA business type, with counts. Not errors in themselves; pairs that cross families (shop against restaurant) are flagged. | 8 |
| AT-5 | attribute conflict | Station and tunnel levels from different sources disagree | Published levels in data/sourced-levels.json grouped by station and line. A depth below ground is turned into m OD with the LiDAR ground at the station node (docklands/data/area.js). Pairs more than 2 m apart are flagged; the control marked superseded_by is the one not applied. | 2 |
| VA-1 | validity | Repeated word in an address | Source address strings (FSA, CWG) with an address word twice in a row ("Unit Unit", "London London"), after the business name is removed from the front of the address (FSA addresses often start with it, and names such as "Tian Tian" repeat on purpose). | 0 |
| VA-2 | validity | Level values our parser cannot read | OSM level tags in the box, read by tools/osm-values.mjs osmLevels (lists with ";" or ",", ranges such as "0-2", fractions such as "0.5"). Issues: tokens it cannot read ("G", "roof"). The breakdown counts the readable values that are not a single whole number. | 0 |
| VA-3 | validity | Postcodes that are malformed, never allocated or terminated | Every postcode on an OSM feature or an FSA premises (OSM lists of several postcodes split on ";"), checked against the UK format and, inside E14, the ONSPD (August 2026). Postcodes outside E14 are checked for format only. | 20 |
| VA-4 | validity | Wikidata numbers with extra tokens | Numeric Wikidata facts in the registry (height, floors) whose stored value is not a single number ("33 1": two statements joined). | 0 |
| PL-1 | pipeline | OSM multi-value tags read as one value by our tools | OSM separates several values with ";" ("E14 9DT;E14 9FQ"). For every OSM feature in the box whose addr:postcode is a list: is each postcode of the list in the registry record that holds the feature (the building itself, or the building it is an occupant of)? A postcode missing there is a link our tools lost. The breakdown counts list values per tag in the source (not errors in themselves). | 0 |
| PL-2 | pipeline | Branch addresses with "Unit Unit" | registry/sources/brands/tools/build-branches.mjs writes "Unit " before OSM addr:unit, and mappers often put "Unit 14" (not "14") in addr:unit. Counted: OSM addr:unit values that already start with "Unit", and branch addresses that carry the doubled word. | 0 |
| NET-1 | routing network | Parts of the walking network not joined to the rest | Connected parts of the OSM walking network in the Canary Wharf area after joining stairs, escalators, lifts and walkable areas; points and named places outside the largest part. | 40 |
| NET-2 | routing network | Two levels joined at one point with no stairs, lift or ramp | OSM nodes shared by ways tagged with different levels where no stair, escalator or lift way meets. Often a podium mapped as level 1 meeting a street at level 0, or an unmapped ramp or escalator. | 192 |
| NET-3 | routing network | Escalators and lifts without their levels | Escalator ways with no level tag (drawn on level 0, so they change no level), lifts with fewer than two levels, and stairs or escalators whose ends could not be oriented. | 30 |
| NET-4 | routing network | Places with no mapped corridor on their own level | Named shops, food and drink, services and platforms attached to the network on a different level from their own, because no path on their level lies within 40 m. | 42 |
| NET-5 | routing network | TfL and OSM number station levels differently | Each TfL step-free station point (GTFS pathways, level index counted from the street) against the level of the nearest OSM network point within 35 m. Pairs that differ show where the two schemes disagree or where OSM lacks the level (the Elizabeth line platforms at Canary Wharf are TfL -4, but the nearest OSM points are at 0). | 22 |
| TM-1 | currency | Food hygiene rating older than three years, or pending | FSA premises in E14 with a rating date before 2023-10-03, or flagged as awaiting a new rating. | 20 |
| TM-2 | currency | Canary Wharf Group directory: age of the copies | Age of each Internet Archive copy used, and of the sitemap date of the page. | 369 |
| TM-3 | currency | Companies registered here that are not active | Company status other than "Active" at Canary Wharf postcodes (liquidation, proposal to strike off, administration and others). | 19 |
| TM-4 | currency | Wikidata occupants and headquarters without dates, or former | Occupant (P466) and headquarters (P159) links used in the registry, against their P580 start and P582 end qualifiers and the organisation's P576 dissolution, fetched in one QLever query by tools/build-categories.mjs (data/raw/registry/wikidata-occupant-classes.json). Former: an end date or a dissolved organisation, still listed as an occupant by the registry. Undated: no qualifier, so a former tenant cannot be told from a current one. | 30 |
| CV-1 | coverage | Fields filled, by entity type | Share of registry buildings and occupants with each attribute. | 0 |
| CV-2 | coverage | Which sources see each branch or directory entry | Source combinations for the 271 chain-store branches; and the CWG directory entries that no other source has: no registry occupant from another source (OSM, FSA, Wikidata, a store page) carries the entry's URL after the branch join and the registry's CWG join (name and place). | 162 |
| CV-3 | coverage | Canary Wharf Group directory entries with no building | Every CWG directory entry after the registry join (tools/build-registry.mjs): linked by the branch join, joined to an occupant by name and place, added as a new occupant, or not placed, with the reason. Also: entries whose joined records (OSM, FSA) sit in different buildings, and entries that match two records of one source in two buildings. | 38 |
| SP-7 | position | Occupant placed by a key, not a position | Occupants that only the CWG directory or a register gives, placed by the building its street address names (high with the postcode, medium without), its mall's host outline (medium: a 2D outline for a mall that runs under several buildings, see SP-6), or the one building (or the one retail building) with its postcode (low). | 30 |
| CV-4 | coverage | Register records in the registry box with no building | Records of the regulatory and public registers (registry/sources/registers/) after the registry join (keys: UPRN, the register point, street address, name at the same postcode, the postcode alone where one building at the postcode centre has it), left out: records outside the registry box and addresses that are not places (SE-3). | 92 |
| ID-5 | identity | Register UPRN that does not identify the place | Register records whose UPRN the join did not use: one UPRN given to records at two or more postcodes in one register (GIAS gives several schools one council UPRN), or a UPRN point over 150 m from the register's own point. | 9 |
| SE-3 | meaning | Register address that is not where the organisation works | Register records whose address the join did not use as a place: GIAS correspondence addresses (overseas schools at 30 Skylines Village), the registered-office service at E14 5HU (5 Churchill Place, 10th floor), care-of and accountant addresses, and charity contact addresses in residential buildings. | 135 |
| SE-1 | meaning | Registered offices concentrated at one address | Postcodes with 100 or more registered companies, and residential buildings with 20 or more. A registered office is a legal address: formation agents, accountants and serviced offices hold thousands. | 36 |
| SE-2 | meaning | Positions that mean "kept here", not "found here" | Heritage records whose coordinate is the holding museum (Wikidata P276) or a 1 km place box (Portable Antiquities Scheme), against all heritage records with a position. | 5888 |

### 25. Provenance export

#### 64. `check-data-register` (tools/check-data-register.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/check-data-register.mjs`
- Method: Checks the data register against the files git tracks (git ls-files) and the pages, and pipeline.json against the tools and files; exits 1 on a problem.
- Rules:
  - Every tracked data file (not .mjs, .py, .c, .html, .gitignore, vendor/, skills/, the register and pipeline files) has a register entry; every entry's file exists.
  - Every source and OSM extract named is defined; osm.use agrees with the sources.
  - A page that loads a file holding OSM data (use raw, derived or counts) shows '© OpenStreetMap contributors' linked to https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright.
  - pipeline.json: every tracked tools/*, tools/native/*, tools/test/*, registry/sources/*/tools/*, registry/sources/*/* and feeds/* script (.mjs, .py, .sh, .c) is an activity, library or test; every tool, committed input and output exists; every source key is defined; every 'after' names an activity; every register file produced by a tools/ script is generated by an activity.
  - The repository paths in data-register.json osm_elsewhere_in_repo exist.
  - Every activity and every hand step (manual_activities) has a non-empty method and non-empty rules, an area that pipeline.json areas defines, and fault ids that the fault register in skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md has; hand steps name only committed files and known sources.
- Inputs:
  - [data-register.json](data-register.json)
  - [pipeline.json](pipeline.json)
  - [index.html](index.html)
  - [atlas/index.html](atlas/index.html)
  - [docklands/index.html](docklands/index.html)
  - [postcodes/index.html](postcodes/index.html)
  - [registry/index.html](registry/index.html)
  - [schematic.html](schematic.html)
  - [feeds/index.html](feeds/index.html)
  - [skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md](skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md)
- Outputs: none (prints only)
- Network: no; deterministic: yes; kind: check; after: nothing
- Note: Child process: git ls-files. Run last, after every rebuild.

#### 65. `check-data-register-write` (tools/check-data-register.mjs)

- Command: `node magpie/cwplans/tools/check-data-register.mjs --write`
- Method: Runs the same checks, then writes the readable register, the method catalogue (METHODS.md) and the provenance as JSON-LD for a knowledge graph.
- Rules:
  - Activities become prov:Activity with prov:used and prov:generated; register files become prov:Entity and dcat:Distribution with prov:wasDerivedFrom their sources and prov:wasGeneratedBy the activity that generates them; sources become dcat:Dataset.
  - Committed files are identified by their GitHub URL, local files by urn:cwplans:local:<path>.
  - METHODS.md: methods-intro.md verbatim, then every activity by area (pipeline.json areas) in rebuild order (topological over after, ties in file order, a cycle in file order), the hand steps, the hand-judgement table, the fault register table read from the curation skill with the activities that cite each fault, the audit checks from quality/issues.json, the rebuild order, libraries and tests.
  - Hand steps (manual_activities) become prov:Activity with cwp:kind hand; area, faults and judgement are exported as cwp:area, cwp:fault and cwp:judgement.
- Inputs:
  - [data-register.json](data-register.json)
  - [pipeline.json](pipeline.json)
  - [methods-intro.md](methods-intro.md)
  - [skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md](skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md)
  - [quality/issues.json](quality/issues.json)
- Outputs:
  - [DATA-REGISTER.md](DATA-REGISTER.md)
  - [pipeline.jsonld](pipeline.jsonld)
  - [METHODS.md](METHODS.md)
- Network: no; deterministic: no; kind: export; after: `check-data-register`
- Note: DATA-REGISTER.md and METHODS.md carry the run date.

#### Hand step `licence-decisions`: Licence check of every source before commit

- By: hand (each session), owner decides open cases; date: 2026-10-02/03
- Method: Before a source is fetched into a committed file, its licence is read on the provider's own page and recorded in data-register.json sources (name, licence, attribution, note). Restricted, proprietary and share-alike sources other than OSM are not committed; open questions wait for the owner.
- Rules:
  - Not committed: the VOA rating list (restricted licence), the PLA ArcGIS layers (no licence stated), the Canal & River Trust layers (no commercial use).
  - Not used: GLA canopy and green cover 2024 (all rights reserved), Curio Canopy (CC BY-SA), Wikipedia text (CC BY-SA), CC BY-SA Commons photos (looked at only).
  - Held for the owner: the Crossrail long section (licence not stated).
  - Needs an account, not fetched: HM Land Registry CCOD and OCOD, EPC data.
  - Re-check the Gambling Commission (no licence on the download page; data.gov.uk says OGL) before anything leaves prototyping.
- Inputs: none
- Outputs:
  - [data-register.json](data-register.json)
- Decisions recorded in: data-register.json (sources); DATA-REGISTER.md; registry/sources/*/README.md (licence sections); registry/sources/SOURCES-companies-property.md

#### Hand step `provenance-upkeep`: Keeping pipeline.json and the register in step with the tools

- By: hand (each session); date: 2026-10-03
- Method: Every new or changed tool updates its pipeline.json activity in the same commit (used, generated, method, rules, network, deterministic, after, area, faults, judgement); every new data file gets a data-register.json entry in the same commit; then check-data-register.mjs --write regenerates DATA-REGISTER.md, METHODS.md and pipeline.jsonld.
- Rules:
  - The check fails on an unlisted tool, an empty method or rules, an unknown area or fault id, an unregistered data file, and a page that shows OSM data without the attribution link.
  - Source identifiers are kept as they come (OSM type/id, QID, UPRN, TOID, FHRS id, company number, cwb- id): they become IRIs.
- Inputs: none
- Outputs:
  - [pipeline.json](pipeline.json)
- Decisions recorded in: pipeline.json; skills/docklands-data-curation/ACTIVITY-LOG.md

## Hand-judgement steps

Where a person (or an agent working by hand) decides, and where the decision is written down.

| step | area | what is judged | where it is recorded |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fetch-works` (tool) | feeds | Zone margins, the rail lines treated as serving the zone, the postcode districts treated as wholly in the zone, the event words of the planning query and the dropped fields (premises names, submitter names, skips and scaffolding) are choices recorded in skills/cwplans-permits-and-works/SKILL.md and feeds/works/README.md. | skills/cwplans-permits-and-works/SKILL.md; feeds/works/README.md |
| `build-data` (tool) | buildings | The published levels in data/sourced-levels.json are chosen by hand (manual activity sourced-levels). | tools/build-data.mjs |
| `build-docklands` (tool) | buildings | The published levels in data/sourced-levels.json are chosen by hand (manual activity sourced-levels); the KEEP class list for Wikidata places is written in the tool. | tools/build-docklands.mjs |
| `registry-voa` (tool) | registry | Licence decision: the VOA rating list is restricted, so neither output is committed (owner to decide). | tools/registry-voa.mjs |
| `match-fsa-fetch` (tool) | brands | A prefix-variant, alternate or type-mismatch match is decided by hand in registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json (manual activity brand-decisions). | registry/sources/brands/tools/match-fsa.mjs |
| `match-fsa` (tool) | brands | Same decisions as match-fsa-fetch (registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json). | registry/sources/brands/tools/match-fsa.mjs |
| `fetch-registers` (tool) | registers | Which registers to take and their licence reading are recorded in registry/sources/registers/README.md (licence notes, rejected sources). | tools/fetch-registers.mjs |
| `facade-measure` (tool) | facades | The facade region in each photo is chosen by eye (registry/sources/facades/work/rois.json); the fundamental period is chosen by looking at the patch; the outputs are collected into meas.json by hand. | registry/sources/facades/tools/facade.py |
| `facade-measure-patch` (tool) | facades | The patch is cut by hand; the outputs are collected into meas.json by hand. | registry/sources/facades/tools/measure.py |
| `facade-build` (tool) | facades | The floor count used to scale a patch is an assumed or judged value where no face width is visible. | registry/sources/facades/tools/build.py |
| `facade-write` (tool) | facades | Judged facts (by looking at photos) and their sources are written in write_facades.py; each value is marked measured, judged or assumed in facades.json. | registry/sources/facades/tools/write_facades.py |
| `build-facade-atlas` (tool) | facades | One patch per tower chosen by eye from data/raw/facades/contact.jpg; Citigroup Centre left out (crane, blur). | tools/build-facade-atlas.py |
| `fetch-sky` (tool) | sky | Source choice and licence decisions (BSC5 as public domain, d3-celestial lines as BSD-3, CelesTrak with no stated licence and flagged for review, Vieira Milky Way outlines refused) are recorded in data-register.json sources and docklands/README.md "Sky, time, weather and tide". | tools/fetch-sky.mjs |
| `walk-london-datastore` (tool) | feeds | The harvest shortlist (the HARVEST table in the tool) was chosen by hand from the ranked triage: open, relevant, new, machine-readable, not geometry-only where an alternative exists; the choices and the ranked backlog with reasons are in feeds/london-datastore/README.md. The probe rules (caps, thresholds of D2 and D4, the place list) were set by reading the first full run: one cell naming Deptford or one publisher postcode (City Hall) made a zone dataset of a payments list, so D2 needs 3 distinct zone postcodes and D4 2 names in 3 cells; census table ids (QS101EW) read as postcodes until the UK postcode area list was added. Fatal fires (24yo6, incident postcodes) was added to the sensitive titles by hand. | tools/walk-london-datastore.mjs |
| `lds-harvest-auto` (tool) | feeds | The hand rules (HAND in the tool) choose resources where the general rules would read too much or the wrong file (per-link emission workbooks, variant zips, superseded releases) and thin the LAEI 20 m concentration grids to 100 m; each has its reason. Datasets of incident records at addresses (LFB incidents, mobilisations, animal rescues) are held with the lift entrapments for the owner. Outputs were read by hand after each batch (counts, zone keys, sizes). | tools/lds-harvest-auto.mjs |
| `amend-uprns` (tool) | feeds | The thresholds (50 m, 150 m, 10 m, 1 m, 25 UPRN points) and the confidence levels are hand choices, recorded in the tool header constants and in skills/cwplans-london-datastore/SKILL.md "Amending rounded UPRNs"; the measured precision that supports them is in the amendments file meta.validation. The 2025 Cultural Infrastructure Map (2rj5o, no licence on the Datastore) was left out by decision. | tools/amend-uprns.mjs |
| `fetch-crown-lighting` (tool) | lighting | Which pages to add to the hand list (KNOWN in the tool) after a web search; recorded in press-pages.json meta.known_pages. | tools/fetch-crown-lighting.mjs |
| `build-trees` (tool) | trees | The 35 m cut-off for "lone trees" that are cranes or structures is applied on the page, not in the file; the source precedence order is a judgement. | tools/build-trees.mjs |
| `fetch-live` (tool) | feeds | Which sources are open enough to commit (TfL terms, CC BY 4.0) and which only as facts (NATS PIB, UK AIP) or not at all (CAA CAP 1455; OpenSky, adsb.fi, airplanes.live, ADS-B Exchange; adsb.lol as ODbL without the owner's agreement) is recorded in data-register.json sources, feeds/live/README.md and the skill cwplans-live-state. | tools/fetch-live.mjs |
| `fetch-river` (tool) | water | Licence classes per source (facts only for PLA, CRT and the operators' pages); the lock rules in feeds/river/locks-facts.json are read and paraphrased by hand (recorded with URL and date); the sonde plausibility thresholds; the choice of stations and sondes. Recorded in skills/cwplans-river-and-water/SKILL.md and feeds/river/README.md. | tools/fetch-river.mjs |
| `build-construction-index` (tool) | construction | The status windows (7, 5 and 2 years), the "major" filter and the match distances are judgements, recorded in the tool header, meta.status_rules and meta.match_rules and the cwplans-construction skill. facts.json is hand-written from crawled pages and the owner's photos (crawl list inside it). | tools/build-construction-index.mjs |
| `fetch-ais` (tool) | water | Owner decision 2026-10-04: AISHub and aisstream.io events are kept for scoping (licence class scoping-accepted-2026-10-04), marked for review before scaling or commercial use. Before that decision: Open Waters re-serves each event under its upstream source's terms. Only open licences were kept (CC0 single volunteer receptions, NLOD, CC BY). AISHub (a private written assurance reported by Open Waters, revocable) and aisstream.io (no terms) are not kept without the owner's agreement; on 2026-10-04 they were the whole vessel coverage of the zone, so 0 vessels were kept and 3 CC0 aids to navigation. | tools/fetch-ais.mjs |
| `walk-portals` (tool) | feeds | The scope lists, the zone place exceptions, the JUDGED tables (held from another source, coarser than the zone) and the HARVEST tables were set by hand from the first triage; the reasons are in the tables and in feeds/portals/README.md. Licences that read 'no restrictions' without naming a licence (the Tower Hamlets ArcGIS Hub) are not treated as open. | tools/walk-portals.mjs |
| `build-branches` (tool) | brands | CWG matches that the rules leave undecided are decided by hand in registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-decisions.json (manual activity brand-decisions). | registry/sources/brands/tools/build-branches.mjs |
| `storelocator` (tool) | brands | Store-page URLs that OSM does not give were found by hand on the brand locators: registry/sources/brands/tools/storelocator-manual.json (manual activity storelocator-manual). | registry/sources/brands/tools/storelocator.mjs |
| `crawl-sites` (tool) | crawl | The skip list (FSA pages, OSM, Wikidata, Companies House, Land Registry, Wikipedia) is a licence and source-of-truth decision written in the tool. | tools/crawl-sites.mjs |
| `render-structured-data` (tool) | structured | The skip list and the consent-button list are written in the tool; store-finder searches follow the owner rule of 2026-10-03. | tools/render-structured-data.mjs |
| `build-categories` (tool) | registry | The rule tables (which OSM, FSA, CWG, register and Wikidata classes give which category) are written in the tool; mixed CWG labels are left out by judgement. | tools/build-categories.mjs |
| `build-towers` (tool) | towers | JUDGEMENT in tools/build-towers.py: the primary survey per tower (2018 for Novotel cwb-0701 and Dollar Bay cwb-0737), levels added by hand (Pan Peninsula cwb-0801: 6.5 and 21.5 m read off the height histogram), a ground level for the height check (8 Canada Square cwb-0417: 9.7 m OD); SKIP lists outlines that are not towers (cwb-0418, cwb-0523). Each reason is in the tool and in the tower record. | tools/build-towers.py |
| `drone-flight` (tool) | splats | The route (overview loop, spiral, dock passes, tower orbits, crossings) is designed by hand in plan(); only the clearance repair is automatic. | tools/drone-flight.mjs |
| `audit-quality` (tool) | quality | Each new check is read by hand against its examples before it is trusted (curation skill, "Catalogue first"). | tools/audit-quality.mjs |
| `discover-feeds` (tool) | feeds | The hand lists (local news, community, councils, public bodies, hashtags, subreddits), the Feedspot slugs, the Common Crawl sample sizes, the kind of each seed and the events.json merge rule are hand decisions written in the tool; recorded in skills/cwplans-feed-discovery/SKILL.md. | tools/discover-feeds.mjs |
| `solve-photo-sun` (tool) | sky | The pixel positions in points.json are read by eye from 2x and 4x enlargements (which point is the foot of a post is a judgement: the base plate hides it); recorded in points.json and the README. | tools/solve-photo-sun.mjs |
| `sourced-levels` (hand step) | underground | Published levels used as tunnel and structure controls | data/sourced-levels.json (source, quote, superseded_by per control) |
| `docklands-facts` (hand step) | underground | Cited facts on the underground and multi-storey structure (The quote-check script and the FACTS.md generator were one-off scripts and are not committed: the method is recorded here, the code is not.) | docklands/facts.json; docklands/FACTS.md |
| `underground-catalogue` (hand step) | underground | Source catalogue for the underground estate (The generator of feeds/underground/README.md is not committed.) | feeds/underground/*-excluded.json |
| `feeds-catalogue` (hand step) | feeds | Catalogue of live feeds, APIs and event sources (The generators of feeds/README.md and feeds/EVENTS.md are not committed ("generated and by hand").) | feeds/feeds.json and feeds/events.json (status, licence, excluded section); feeds/SURVEY-2026-10-03.md |
| `brand-decisions` (hand step) | brands | Hand decisions on FSA and CWG brand matches | registry/sources/brands/tools/fsa-decisions.json; registry/sources/brands/tools/cwg-decisions.json |
| `storelocator-manual` (hand step) | brands | Store-page URLs found by hand | registry/sources/brands/tools/storelocator-manual.json |
| `records-open-subset` (hand step) | heritage | Open-licence subset of the museum and heritage records (The cut was made by hand; no script in the repository writes records-open.json.) | registry/sources/museums/README.md, "Published subset" |
| `ukho-wrecks-clip` (hand step) | water | UKHO wrecks and obstructions clipped to the box | registry/sources/pla/README.md |
| `facade-photos` (hand step) | facades | Open photos, facade regions and footprint widths for the facade measures | registry/sources/facades/photos.json (face and how it was judged); registry/sources/facades/work/rois.json |
| `page-assets` (hand step) | imagery | Pixel-art palette and music list for the 3D page (The palette extraction was a one-off script and is not committed.) | docklands/data/pixel-palette.json; docklands/data/music.json |
| `licence-decisions` (hand step) | provenance | Licence check of every source before commit | data-register.json (sources); DATA-REGISTER.md; registry/sources/*/README.md (licence sections); registry/sources/SOURCES-companies-property.md |
| `provenance-upkeep` (hand step) | provenance | Keeping pipeline.json and the register in step with the tools | pipeline.json; skills/docklands-data-curation/ACTIVITY-LOG.md |
| `quality-analysis` (hand step) | quality | Analysis of the error classes and the fault register | quality/README.md; skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md (Fault register); skills/docklands-data-curation/ACTIVITY-LOG.md |
| `crown-lighting-judgement` (hand step) | lighting | Crown lighting policy, campaigns and dated colour observations (The pixel measurements were run from the session scratchpad (a 10-line Pillow script: crop, sort by brightness, mean of the top fraction); the boxes are in no committed file.) | registry/sources/lighting/crown-lighting.json (read, confidence, hex_kind per record); skills/cwplans-crown-lighting/SKILL.md |

## Fault register

Faults found in this project's joins and tools (F-numbers). Evidence and audit counts: [the curation skill, Fault register](https://github.com/danbri/glitchcan-minigam/blob/master/magpie/cwplans/skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md#fault-register) ([local copy](skills/docklands-data-curation/SKILL.md)). Add a new fault there with the next number, and name it in the "faults" of the activities it touches.

| id | found | class | status | fault | activities |

| F2 | 2026-10-03 | pipeline | fixed: `tools/osm-values.mjs` `osmList`, fixture tests in `tools/test/`. | F32 | 2026-10-04 | currency (source) | rule in use in `tools/build-construction-index.mjs`: such a site is "commenced long ago" (S2, low) unless another source shows work now (S2b, on site, medium) | Planning London Datahub (LDD) commencements never closed: 158 site groups commenced more than 7 years ago and have no completion date (Baltimore Wharf 2008, Westferry Printworks 2016); 27 of them show construction now (OSM, NOTAM or Street Manager), 131 show nothing | `build-construction-index` |
| F33 | 2026-10-04 | currency (source) | rule in use: S4b (a crane NOTAM within 60 m or an OSM building=construction outline makes an approved site "on site", medium); a commenced record is never dropped for a past `lapsed_date` | PLD dates lag and one is computed: 4 approved sites with a NOTAM crane or an OSM building under construction have no commencement (60 Gracechurch Street, 75 London Wall, TW2 Blackwall, G Park Docklands); 44 of 2,908 commenced records started after their `lapsed_date` (30 Marsh Wall: lapses 2025-07-28, commenced 2025-10-03), so `lapsed_date` is decision + permission period, not an observation | `build-construction-index` |
| F34 | 2026-10-04 | currency (source) | rule in use: Wikidata state is shown on the site record and never used for the status | Wikidata "state of use: under construction" (P5817 Q12377751) on finished buildings: Landmark Pinnacle (Q16258428, completed 2020) and South Quay Plaza (Q21585412), 2 of the 4 such items in the zone | `build-construction-index` |
| F35 | 2026-10-04 | geometry (source) | rule in use: a polygon under 50 m2 is no polygon; the footprint falls back to OSM construction outlines, then a circle of the stated site area, then 25 m, and `footprint.from` says which | PLD polygons that are point markers: of 5,127 major zone records 1,923 have a square of a few metres round the centroid and 1,401 none (1,803 real); the PLD gives no storeys for 30 Marsh Wall or 25 Cuba | `build-construction-index` |

## Rebuild order

All tool activities in an order that satisfies every "after" (the brands merge and the store-page check depend on each other: build-branches runs again after storelocator). Commands run from the repository root. Network steps need `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1` behind the proxy.

1. `fetch-works` (feeds, network): `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-works.mjs [tfl-lines tfl-bus tfl-road street-manager gazette th-licences planning markets venue-events] [--no-fetch] [--refresh] [--days=90] [--month=YYYY-MM]`
2. `fetch-raw-grid` (terrain, network): `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-raw.mjs grid`
3. `fetch-raw-osm` (buildings, network): `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-raw.mjs osm`
4. `fetch-raw-lidar` (terrain, network): `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-raw.mjs lidar`
5. `fetch-raw-wikidata` (buildings, network): `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-raw.mjs wikidata`
6. `fetch-docklands-osm` (buildings, network): `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-docklands.mjs osm`
7. `fetch-docklands-lidar` (terrain, network): `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-docklands.mjs lidar`
8. `fetch-docklands-defences` (water, network): `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-docklands.mjs defences`
9. `fetch-docklands-wikidata` (buildings, network): `node magpie/cwplans/tools/fetch-docklands.mjs wikidata`
10. `fetch_wet-soundings` (water, network): `python3 magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/pla/fetch_wet.py soundings`
11. `fetch_wet-layers` (water, network): `python3 magpie/cwplans/registry/sources/pla/fetch_wet.py layers`
